; ZX Spectrum Next ROM Disassembly (ROM 0)
; =======================================================================================================================
; File: enNextZX.rom
; Source ROM Block: ROM 0 (file bytes 0x0000-0x3FFF)
;=======================================================================================================================

#include "next-symbols.asm"

;
; RST $00 / reset entry.
; Disable interrupts immediately, then jump over the ROM identification bytes
; to the cold-start initialization routine at ColdStart.
;
    di                          ; 0000 F3           Keep interrupts disabled during early hardware setup
    jp ColdStart                ; 0001 C3 EF 00     Start ROM0 cold initialization
    .defb $45, $44, $08, $02    ; 0004 45 44 08 02  "E", "D", 08, 02
;
; RST $08:
;
    jp L15E0                    ; 0008 C3 E0 15   
    .defb $2A, $2E, $2A, $FF    ; 000B 2A 2E 2A FF  "*", ".", "*", copyright
    .defb $00                   ; 000F 00           00
; 
; RST $08: Page in RAM bank 7 and switch to work area stack
; This routine pages RAM bank 7 into slot 7 ($C000-$FFFF) and switches the stack pointer
; to the work area near system variables (TMPSP, typically $5BFF). This provides a workspace
; with RAM at $C000 and a stack below the system variables area at $5C00.
; Entry: None
; Exit: RAM bank 7 paged at $C000, SP switched to TMPSP area, continues at L15E0
;
    rst $28                     ; 0010 EF         
    .defw $0010                 ; 0011 10 00      
L0013
    ret                         ; 0013 C9         
    jp ColdStart                ; 0014 C3 EF 00   
    .defb $00                   ; 0017 00         
;
; RST $18: Call ROM1 routine. The address to call is specified in the stack.
;                      nop
    jp Rom1Call                 ; 0018 C3 80 3E   
    .defb $3C, $44, $49, $52    ; 001B 3C 44 49 52  Filler bytes: "<DIR"
    .defb $BE                   ; 001F BE           $BE
;
; RST $20: Call ROM2 routine. The address to call is specified in the stack.
;
    jp Rom2Call                 ; 0020 C3 00 3E   
    .defb $14, $00, $04, $98    ; 0023 14 00 04 98
    .defb $00                   ; 0027 00         
;
; RST $28: Call ROM3 routine. The address to call is specified in the stack.
;
    ld (TMPBC),bc               ; 0028 ED 43 54 5B  Save the value of BC temporarily
    ex (sp),hl                  ; 002C E3           Move the return address to HL
    jp Rom3Cont                 ; 002D C3 80 00   
;
; RST $30
;
    jp L15C9                    ; 0030 C3 C9 15   
    call L04D7                  ; 0033 CD D7 04   
    ld (hl),a                   ; 0036 77         
    ret                         ; 0037 C9         
;
; RST $38: IRQ handler
;
IRQ
    push af                     ; 0038 F5         
    push hl                     ; 0039 E5           Save used registers (AF, HL)
    ld h,$00                    ; 003A 26 00        DivMMC value at return
    ld a,$80                    ; 003C 3E 80        DivMMC value at start
    jp `Irq1                    ; 003E C3 46 00   
;
; Unused filler bytes
;
    .defb $3C, $52, $55, $4E    ; 0041 3C 52 55 4E  Fillers bytes "<RUN"
    .defb $BE                   ; 0045 BE         
; Continuation of IRQ handler
;
`Irq1
    out ($E3),a                 ; 0046 D3 E3        Turns on DivMMC ROM and RAM page 0
;
; Unused filler bytes
;
    .defb $05, $1a, $17, $ff    ; 0048 05 1A 17 FF
    .defb $07, $87, $00, $00    ; 004C 07 87 00 00
    .defb $00, $00, $00, $00    ; 0050 00 00 00 00
    .defb $00, $00, $00, $00    ; 0054 00 00 00 00
    .defb $00, $00, $00, $00    ; 0058 00 00 00 00
    .defb $00, $00, $00, $00    ; 005C 00 00 00 00
;
; With DivMMC ROM turned off, this will be the address
;
`Irq2
    pop hl                      ; 0060 E1           Restore used registers
    pop af                      ; 0061 F1         
    ei                          ; 0062 FB           Enable the IRQ again
    ret                         ; 0063 C9           Done
;
; Filler bytes
;
    .defb $00, $00              ; 0064 00 00      
;
; NMI routine
;
NMI
    retn                        ; 0066 ED 45        ROM0 does not do anything with NMI
;
; Call an AltROM routine with an inline target address.
; The two bytes after the CALL are the AltROM target address. The trampoline
; preserves BC, skips the inline operand for the ROM0 return address, enables
; AltROM for reads, and jumps to the target by returning through the stack.
; AltROM has a matching $007B return stub that disables AltROM again.
; Entry: (SP) = address of inline target operand.
; Exit: Returns after the AltROM routine; BC preserved.
;
ALTROM_CALL
    ld (TMPBC),bc               ; 0068 ED 43 54 5B  Save the value of BC temporarily
    ex (sp),hl                  ; 006C E3           HL := Return address
    ld c,(hl)                   ; 006D 4E         
    inc hl                      ; 006E 23         
    ld b,(hl)                   ; 006F 46           Read the 16-bit value from the stack into BC
    inc hl                      ; 0070 23           Set the return address
    ex (sp),hl                  ; 0071 E3           to the next one
; Entry: BC = AltROM target address. Used after ALTROM_CALL has saved BC, and
; also by dispatch code that has already loaded an AltROM target into BC.
ALTROM_CALL_BC
    push $007B                  ; 0072 ED 8A 00 7B  Use the $007B address of the alternate ROM to return
    push bc                     ; 0076 C5           Execute routine addressed with BC in the alternate ROM
    ld bc,(TMPBC)               ; 0077 ED 4B 54 5B  Restore the original value of BC
    nextreg $8C,$80             ; 007B ED 91 8C 80  Enable AltROM (for reads)
    ret                         ; 007F C9         
;
; RST $28 Continuation: Call ROM3 Routine
; This is the target of RST $28 (JP Rom3Cont at $002D). RST $28 has already saved BC to TMPBC
; and loaded HL with the return address. This routine reads the inline 16-bit target address,
; adjusts the return address, and jumps to L5B48 (RAM routine) which switches to ROM3 and
; executes the target routine. Returns via $5B4D which switches back to ROM0.
; Entry: HL = return address (pointing to inline target address), BC saved in TMPBC
; Exit: Executes ROM3 routine and returns to adjusted return address, BC preserved
;                 
Rom3Cont
    ld c,(hl)                   ; 0080 4E           When entering, HL is the return address of the caller
    inc hl                      ; 0081 23         
    ld b,(hl)                   ; 0082 46           Read the 16-bit value from the stack into BC
    inc hl                      ; 0083 23           Set the return address
    ex (sp),hl                  ; 0084 E3           to the next one
L0085
    push $5B4D                  ; 0085 ED 8A 5B 4D  Return to the RAM routine that switches back ROM0
    push bc                     ; 0089 C5           Push the original return address to the stack
    ld bc,(TMPBC)               ; 008A ED 4B 54 5B  Restore the original value of BC
    jp L5B48                    ; 008E C3 48 5B     Jump to the RAM routine that turns on ROM3
;
; Pager subroutines
; The CopyPages subroutine moves these routines to $5B00-$5B51
;
; PagerStart: ZX Spectrum +2/+3 type paging flips ROMs:
; ROM0 -> ROM3
; ROM1 -> ROM2
; ROM2 -> ROM1
; ROM3 -> ROM0
;
; Address in RAM: $5B00
;
PagerStart
    push af                     ; 0091 F5         
    push bc                     ; 0092 C5           Save used registers (AF, BC)
    ld bc,$7FFD                 ; 0093 01 FD 7F     ZX Spectrum 128 port
    ld a,($5B5C)                ; 0096 3A 5C 5B     Get the last value written to $7FFD
    xor $10                     ; 0099 EE 10        Flip ROM bit low
    di                          ; 009B F3           No interrupt while paging
    ld ($5B5C),a                ; 009C 32 5C 5B     Store the last value written back
    out (c),a                   ; 009F ED 79        Switch ROM bit high
RomHSw
    ld bc,$1FFD                 ; 00A1 01 FD 1F     ZX Spectrum +2/+3 port
    ld a,($5B67)                ; 00A4 3A 67 5B     Get the last value written to $1FFD
    xor $04                     ; 00A7 EE 04        Fip ROM bit high
    ld ($5B67),a                ; 00A9 32 67 5B     Store the last value written back
    out (c),a                   ; 00AC ED 79        Switch ROM bit low
    ei                          ; 00AE FB           Enable interrupt again
    pop bc                      ; 00AF C1           Restore used registers
    pop af                      ; 00B0 F1         
    ret                         ; 00B1 C9           Done
;
; This subroutine ($5B21) switches the current ROM and returns th the 
; address stored in $5B5A. Set's the contents of HL as the next return address.
;
; Address in RAM: $5B21
;
    call L5B00                  ; 00B2 CD 00 5B     Calls PagerStart in RAM
    push hl                     ; 00B5 E5           Save HL on stack
    ld hl,($5B5A)               ; 00B6 2A 5A 5B     Load return address from system variable
    ex (sp),hl                  ; 00B9 E3           Swap HL with top of stack
    ret                         ; 00BA C9           Return to the address now in HL
;
; Address in RAM: $5B2A
;
    push hl                     ; 00BB E5           Save HL on stack
    ld hl,$5B34                 ; 00BC 21 34 5B     Set return address to $5B34 (RetFromSw)
    ex (sp),hl                  ; 00BF E3           Swap HL with top of stack
    push af                     ; 00C0 F5         
    push bc                     ; 00C1 C5           Save used registers (AF, BC)
    jp L5B10                    ; 00C2 C3 10 5B     Jump to paging routine at $5B10 (RomHSw)
;
; Returns from the swith
; Address in RAM: $5B34
;
RetFromSw
    push hl                     ; 00C5 E5           Save HL on stack
    ld hl,($5B5A)               ; 00C6 2A 5A 5B     Load return address from system variable
    ex (sp),hl                  ; 00C9 E3           Swap HL with top of stack
    ret                         ; 00CA C9           Return to the address now in HL
;
; This subroutine jumps to $0AB0 in ROM1. It is used widely in ROM1
; Address in RAM: $5B3A
;
    push $0AB0                  ; 00CB ED 8A 0A B0  Push return address $0AB0 onto stack
    nextreg $8E,$01             ; 00CF ED 91 8E 01  Set Next register $8E to $01 (Transparency fallback color)
    ret                         ; 00D3 C9           Done
;
; This subroutine turns to ROM2 and jumps to the top stack address
; Address in RAM: $5B43
;
    nextreg $8E,$02             ; 00D4 ED 91 8E 02  Set Next register $8E to $02 (Transparency fallback color)
    ret                         ; 00D8 C9         
;
; This subroutine turns to ROM3 and jumps to the top stack address.
; It is used widely in ROM3.
; Address in RAM: $5B48
;
    nextreg $8E,$03             ; 00D9 ED 91 8E 03
    ret                         ; 00DD C9         
;
; This subroutine turns to ROM0 and jumps to the top stack address.
; Address in RAM: $5B4D
;
    nextreg $8E,$00             ; 00DE ED 91 8E 00
    ret                         ; 00E2 C9         
;
; Copies the pager routines to $5B00-$5B51
;
CopyPager
    ld hl,$0091                 ; 00E3 21 91 00   
    ld de,$5B00                 ; 00E6 11 00 5B   
;
; Moves $52 bytes
;
    ld bc,CopyPager - PagerStart; 00E9 01 52 00   
    ldir                        ; 00EC ED B0      
    ret                         ; 00EE C9         
;
; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
; Cold-start initialization.
; Entry path from RST $00. This code puts the Next into a known machine,
; bus, MMU, screen, and memory-test state before handing off to the later
; ROM/system-variable initialization code.
; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
;
ColdStart
    nextreg $07,$03             ; 00EF ED 91 07 03  Set CPU speed to 28 MHz (bits 1:0 = 11)
    nextreg $03,$B0             ; 00F3 ED 91 03 B0  Disable bootrom; allow timing change; select +2A/+2B/+3 display timing
    nextreg $C0,$08             ; 00F7 ED 91 C0 08  Interrupt control: stackless NMI enabled (bit 3=1), pulse mode (bit 0=0)
    ld a,$FF                    ; 00FB 3E FF        Load $FF into A for port decoding setup
    nextreg $82,a               ; 00FD ED 92 82     Internal Port Decoding: Enable all internal ports (all bits = 1)
    nextreg $83,a               ; 0100 ED 92 83     Internal Port Decoding byte 2: Enable all (all bits = 1)
    nextreg $84,a               ; 0103 ED 92 84     Internal Port Decoding byte 3: Enable all (all bits = 1)
    nextreg $85,a               ; 0106 ED 92 85     Internal Port Decoding byte 4 (MSB): Enable all (all bits = 1)
    xor a                       ; 0109 AF           Clear A (set to $00)
    nextreg $80,a               ; 010A ED 92 80     Expansion Bus: Disable expansion bus and all overrides
    nextreg $81,a               ; 010D ED 92 81     Expansion Bus Control: Default settings (3.5MHz max)
    nextreg $8A,a               ; 0110 ED 92 8A     Expansion Bus IO Propagate: No port propagation to expansion bus
    nextreg $8F,a               ; 0113 ED 92 8F     Memory Mapping Mode: Standard ZX 128K +3 mode (bits 1:0 = 00)
;
; Keep only selected Peripheral 2 setting bits from nextreg $06 while clearing
; the other hotkey/NMI/audio mode flags for early startup.
; Preserved bits: bit 6 = divert BEEP to internal speaker, bit 2 = PS/2 mode.
;
    ld bc,$243B                 ; 0116 01 3B 24     BC = $243B (nextreg select port)
    ld d,$06                    ; 0119 16 06        D = $06 for peripheral settings
    out (c),d                   ; 011B ED 51        Select nextreg $06
    inc b                       ; 011D 04           BC = $253B (nextreg data port)
    in a,(c)                    ; 011E ED 78        Read current peripheral 2 settings
    and $44                     ; 0120 E6 44        Keep bit 6 (BEEP routing) and bit 2 (PS/2 mode)
    out (c),a                   ; 0122 ED 79        Write back masked settings
;
; Set BLACK paper and BLACK ink for the entire screen
;
    ld hl,$5800                 ; 0124 21 00 58     HL = start of screen attributes
    ld de,$5801                 ; 0127 11 01 58     DE = second byte of attributes
    ld (hl),l                   ; 012A 75           Fill first attribute byte with $00
    ld bc,$02FF                 ; 012B 01 FF 02     BC = 767 bytes to copy
    ldir                        ; 012E ED B0        Clear all screen attributes to $00
;
; First RAM pass.
; B is the number of 16K banks to scan ($70 on a 2MB Next), C is the current
; 16K bank number. Each 16K bank is mapped into MMU slots 6/7 by writing the
; corresponding 8K page pair to nextregs $56/$57.
;
; The loop saves each bank's original last byte into a temporary buffer at
; $4000 upward, writes $BB to the bank's last byte as a presence marker, and
; clears low banks so startup begins from a predictable RAM state. For banks
; that will be cleared, the saved restore byte is deliberately changed to $00
; so the second pass removes the $BB marker without restoring stale contents.
; 
    ld bc,$7000                 ; 0130 01 00 70     BC = $7000 (loop counter for RAM exercise)
    ld hl,$4000                 ; 0133 21 00 40     HL = temporary buffer in screen bitmap memory
; 
; Page the 16K bank indexed by C into MMU slot 6/7
;
`BANK_LOOP
    ld a,c                      ; 0136 79           A := current 16K bank number
    exx                         ; 0137 D9           Switch to registers holding mapped-bank pointers
    add a,a                     ; 0138 87           A := A*2 -> compute 8K page number (word index)
    nextreg $56,a               ; 0139 ED 92 56     Map 8K page to MMU slot 6 ($C000-$DFFF)
    inc a                       ; 013C 3C           Next page (for slot 7)
    nextreg $57,a               ; 013D ED 92 57     Map 8K page to MMU slot 7 ($E000-$FFFF)
    srl a                       ; 0140 CB 3F        A := current 16K bank number again
;
; Save the original last byte of the mapped bank into the temporary buffer.
;
    ld hl,$FFFF                 ; 0142 21 FF FF     HL := last byte of the mapped 16K bank
    ex af,af'                   ; 0145 08           Save current bank number in A'
    ld a,(hl)                   ; 0146 7E           Read original last byte from mapped bank
    exx                         ; 0147 D9           Return to the temp-buffer pointer
    ld (hl),a                   ; 0148 77           Save original last byte in the temp buffer
;
; Banks below 12 are zero-filled below. Replace their saved last-byte value
; with $00, so the second pass restores $00 over the temporary $BB marker.
; Banks 12 and above are not cleared, so their original last byte is preserved.
;
    ld a,c                      ; 0149 79           A := current 16K bank number
    cp $0C                      ; 014A FE 0C        Compare with 12 (special-case threshold)
    jr nc,`KEEP_RESTORE         ; 014C 30 02        If bank >=12, keep the original restore byte
    ld (hl),$00                 ; 014E 36 00        For cleared banks, restore $00 over the marker later
`KEEP_RESTORE
    inc hl                      ; 0150 23           Advance HL pointer
    exx                         ; 0151 D9           Switch to alternate registers
    ex af,af'                   ; 0152 08           Restore current 16K bank number in A
    ld (hl),$BB                 ; 0153 36 BB        Set the last byte of the bank to $BB
    dec hl                      ; 0155 2B           Move HL back (prepare for block op)
    ld de,$FFFD                 ; 0156 11 FD FF     DE := destination for backward clear/fill
    ld bc,$3FFE                 ; 0159 01 FE 3F     BC := byte count for the backward clear/fill
;
; Special cases:
; - Do not zero banks 12 and above.
; - For bank 8, zero only the upper 8K half.
; The Memory Map identifies the early banks as standard RAM, ULA/shadow screen,
; and default Layer 2/NextZXOS RAM areas; this pass gives those startup-critical
; banks known contents while avoiding later banks.
;
    cp $0C                      ; 015C FE 0C        Compare A with 12 again
    jr nc,`NEXT_BANK            ; 015E 30 0A        If bank >=12, skip clearing this bank
    cp $08                      ; 0160 FE 08        Compare A with 8
    jr nz,`DO_CLEAR             ; 0162 20 02        If not bank 8, use the full clear count
    ld b,$1F                    ; 0164 06 1F        Set B := $1F for special-case count
`DO_CLEAR
    ld (hl),$00                 ; 0166 36 00        Write $00 (zero) at current HL
    lddr                        ; 0168 ED B8        Zero the entire bank (or in special case, the upper half)
;
; Move to the next bank
;
`NEXT_BANK
    exx                         ; 016A D9           Restore registers
    inc c                       ; 016B 0C           Move to the next 16K bank number
    djnz `BANK_LOOP             ; 016C 10 C8        Loop until all candidate banks are scanned
    jr RAM_TEST_PASS2           ; 016E 18 1E        Continue with the bit-level RAM test
;
; RAM bit-test failure handler.
; Entry from the two checks below:
; - Z set: the tested bit could not be read back as 1 after writing mask D.
; - Z reset: the tested bit could not be cleared after writing the complement.
;
; E still contains the countdown for bits remaining, so 8-E gives the faulty
; bit index in the range 0..7. The border is then used as a non-returning visual
; diagnostic because normal startup cannot continue safely.
;
INIT_ERR
    ex af,af'                   ; 0170 08           Preserve the failing test flags in AF'
    ld a,$08                    ; 0171 3E 08        A := number of bits tested per bank
    sub e                       ; 0173 93           A := faulty bit index (0..7)
    ld l,a                      ; 0174 6F           L := faulty bit index / border color
    ex af,af'                   ; 0175 08           Restore failing flags for the stuck-high/low branch
    ld a,l                      ; 0176 7D           A := faulty bit index without changing flags
    jr nz,`BIT_ERR              ; 0177 20 04        Z reset: bit stayed 1 after writing its complement
;
; Bit stuck low: show the faulty bit index as a static border color.
;
    out ($FE),a                 ; 0179 D3 FE        Set border color to the faulty bit index
B0_TRAP
    jr B0_TRAP                  ; 017B 18 FE        Stop here; interrupts are still disabled
;
; Bit stuck high: alternate a short complement-color stripe with a longer
; faulty-bit-color stripe.
;
`BIT_ERR
    xor $07                     ; 017D EE 07        H color := 7 - faulty bit index
    ld h,a                      ; 017F 67           H := complement border color
B1_7_TRAP
    ld b,$20                    ; 0180 06 20        Short stripe length
`PULSE_1
    ld a,h                      ; 0182 7C           A := complement border color
    out ($FE),a                 ; 0183 D3 FE        Draw short complement-color stripe
    djnz `PULSE_1               ; 0185 10 FB        Repeat 32 times
`PULSE_2
    ld a,l                      ; 0187 7D           A := faulty bit index / border color
    out ($FE),a                 ; 0188 D3 FE        Draw long faulty-bit-color stripe
    djnz `PULSE_2               ; 018A 10 FB        B wrapped from 0, so repeat 256 times
    jr B1_7_TRAP                ; 018C 18 F2        Repeat the diagnostic pattern forever
;
; Second RAM pass.
; Walk the banks again, detect the $BB presence marker, restore saved last
; bytes, and test each bit at address $DCBA in the mapped bank.
;
RAM_TEST_PASS2
    xor a                       ; 018E AF           A=0: start mapping/test loop for pages
;
; BC points at the screen buffer containing the saved last byte from each bank.
; D is the current one-bit mask, E is the remaining bit count.
;
    ld bc,$4000                 ; 018F 01 00 40     BC -> saved-byte buffer in screen bitmap memory
TEST_RAM_BANK
    ld de,$0108                 ; 0192 11 08 01     D := bit mask $01, E := 8 bits to test
;
; Page in the bank indexed by A
;
    add a,a                     ; 0195 87           A := A*2 (compute 8K page index for nextreg)
    nextreg $56,a               ; 0196 ED 92 56     map computed page into MMU slot 6
    inc a                       ; 0199 3C           prepare next page for slot 7
    nextreg $57,a               ; 019A ED 92 57     map next page into MMU slot 7
    srl a                       ; 019D CB 3F        Restore current 16K bank number
    ex af,af'                   ; 019F 08           Save the bank index to A'
;
; If the mapped bank exists, the first pass left $BB at its last byte.
; If the marker is absent, this bank is not available and A' identifies the
; first unavailable bank.
;
    ld hl,$FFFF                 ; 01A0 21 FF FF     HL := last byte of the mapped 16K bank
    ld a,(hl)                   ; 01A3 7E           Read presence marker from mapped bank
    cp $BB                      ; 01A4 FE BB        Is this a bank marked by the first pass?
    jr nz,RAM_TEST_DONE         ; 01A6 20 24        Marker absent: no more available RAM banks
;
; The bank exists. Restore its original last byte from the screen buffer.
;
    ld a,(bc)                   ; 01A8 0A           Load saved original last byte
    inc bc                      ; 01A9 03           Advance saved-byte buffer pointer
    ld (hl),a                   ; 01AA 77           Restore original last byte to the mapped bank
;
; Use offset $1CBA within the mapped 16K bank ($DCBA in slots 6/7) for the
; destructive bit test. Preserve the original byte in XL.
;
    ld hl,$DCBA                 ; 01AB 21 BA DC     HL := bit-test address within the mapped bank
    ld a,(hl)                   ; 01AE 7E           Read original byte from the test address
    ld xl,a                     ; 01AF DD 6F        Save original test byte in XL
;
; Check that the current test bit can be written and read back as 1.
;
`BIT_LOOP
    ld a,d                      ; 01B1 7A           A := current one-bit test mask
    ld (hl),a                   ; 01B2 77           Write the one-bit mask to the test address
    ld a,(hl)                   ; 01B3 7E           Read the value back
    and d                       ; 01B4 A2           Check whether the tested bit is set
    jr z,INIT_ERR               ; 01B5 28 B9        Error: tested bit read back as 0
;
; Check that the same bit can be cleared by writing the complemented mask.
;
    cpl                         ; 01B7 2F           Invert the read-back pattern
    ld (hl),a                   ; 01B8 77           Write complemented pattern to the test address
    ld a,(hl)                   ; 01B9 7E           Read the value back
    and d                       ; 01BA A2           Check whether the tested bit is clear
    jr nz,INIT_ERR              ; 01BB 20 B3        Error: tested bit read back as 1
;
; Move to the next bit pattern
;
    rlc d                       ; 01BD CB 02        Move mask to the next bit
    dec e                       ; 01BF 1D           One fewer bit left to test
    jr nz,`BIT_LOOP             ; 01C0 20 EF        Loop until all 8 bits are tested
    ld a,xl                     ; 01C2 DD 7D        Restore original test byte from XL
    ld (hl),a                   ; 01C4 77           Write back the original byte at $DCBA
;
; Advance to the next bank and repeat until all possible 16K banks have been
; tested or an unavailable bank is found.
;
    ex af,af'                   ; 01C5 08           Restore current bank number from A'
    inc a                       ; 01C6 3C           Advance to the next 16K bank number
    cp $70                      ; 01C7 FE 70        Have all 112 candidate 16K banks been tested?
    jr nz,TEST_RAM_BANK         ; 01C9 20 C7        No: continue with the next bank
;
; All candidate 16K RAM banks up to $6F were present and passed the bit test.
;
    ex af,af'                   ; 01CB 08           Put terminal bank value in A'
;
; A' contains either $70 (all possible banks tested) or the first unavailable
; bank. Store the previous bank number as LBANK.
;
RAM_TEST_DONE
    ex af,af'                   ; 01CC 08           A := terminal/first-unavailable bank number
    dec a                       ; 01CD 3D           A := last available bank number
    ld (LBANK),a                ; 01CE 32 69 5B     Record last available RAM bank
    ld sp,$5BFF                 ; 01D1 31 FF 5B     Set startup stack below system variables
;
; Continue with ROM and system-variable initialization.
;
    rst $20                     ; 01D4 E7           Call ROM2 routine $1EDF
    .defw $1EDF                 ; 01D5 DF 1E      
    nextreg $8e,$08             ; 01D7 ED 91 8E 08  Select ROM0 and RAM bank 0 in 128K mapping
    im 1                        ; 01DB ED 56        Set interrupt mode 1
    call CopyPager              ; 01DD CD E3 00     Copy ROM paging trampolines to RAM
    ld hl,$FFFF                 ; 01E0 21 FF FF     HL := top of physical RAM
    ld ($5CB4),hl               ; 01E3 22 B4 5C     Set PRAMT to $FFFF
    ld de,$3EAF                 ; 01E6 11 AF 3E     DE := last byte of ROM3 UDG definitions
    ld bc,$00A8                 ; 01E9 01 A8 00     BC := $A8 bytes to copy from ROM3
    ex de,hl                    ; 01EC EB         
    rst $28                     ; 01ED EF           Call ROM3 LDDR-style copy routine
    .defw $1661                 ; 01EE 61 16      
    ex de,hl                    ; 01F0 EB         
    inc hl                      ; 01F1 23         
    ld (UDG),hl                 ; 01F2 22 7B 5C     Set the UDG system variable to the beginning of the copied area
    dec hl                      ; 01F5 2B         
    ld ($5CB2),hl               ; 01F6 22 B2 5C     Set RAMTOP to the address before UDG
    ld b,$00                    ; 01F9 06 00      
    ld iy,$5C3A                 ; 01FB FD 21 3A 5C  IY := ERR_NR, the Spectrum sysvar base
    ld a,(LBANK)                ; 01FF 3A 69 5B     Get the index of the last available RAM bank
    ld c,a                      ; 0202 4F         
    ld hl,(UDG)                 ; 0203 2A 7B 5C     HL := UDG address
    exx                         ; 0206 D9           Save main registers
;
; Configure initial DivMMC settings
;
    nextreg $B8,$82             ; 0207 ED 91 B8 82  Enable DivMMC automap on $0038 and $0008
    nextreg $B9,$00             ; 020B ED 91 B9 00  Make those entry points valid only in ROM3
    nextreg $BA,$00             ; 020F ED 91 BA 00  Use delayed mapping for those entry points
    nextreg $BB,$F2             ; 0213 ED 91 BB F2  Enable $3Dxx automap and disable $1FF8-$1FFF
                                                           ; Enable $056A, $04D7, and instant $0066 automap
    nextreg $D8,$01             ; 0217 ED 91 D8 01  Enable +3 FDC traps on ports $2FFD and $3FFD
;
; Init peripheral 1 settings (Reg $05)
;
    ld a,$05                    ; 021B 3E 05      
    call GetNxRegA              ; 021D CD DE 12   
    and $05                     ; 0220 E6 05        Keep 50/60Hz mode and scandoubler settings only
    or $5A                      ; 0222 F6 5A        Set joystick 1 and 2 mode to MD1
    out (c),a                   ; 0224 ED 79        Write out the new port value
;
; Init peripheral 3 settings (Reg $08)
;
    ld a,$08                    ; 0226 3E 08      
    call GetNxRegA              ; 0228 CD DE 12   
    or $4E                      ; 022B F6 4E        Disable RAM and port contention
                                                           ; Enable 8-bit DACs (A,B,C,D)
                                                           ; Enable port $FF Timex video mode read
                                                           ; Enable turbosound
    out (c),a                   ; 022D ED 79        Write out the new port value
;
; Init peripheral 2 settings (Reg $06)
;
    ld a,$06                    ; 022F 3E 06      
    call GetNxRegA              ; 0231 CD DE 12   
    and $44                     ; 0234 E6 44        Keep BEEP routing and PS/2 mode bits only
    or $AB                      ; 0236 F6 AB        Enable F8 cpu speed hotkey and F5/F6 expansion bus hotkeys
                                                           ; Enable F3 hotkey, M1 NMI, and hold AY in reset
    out (c),a                   ; 0238 ED 79        Write out the new port value
    and $FC                     ; 023A E6 FC        Set YM audio chip mode
    out (c),a                   ; 023C ED 79        Write out the new port value
;
; Init peripheral 5 settings (Reg $0A)
;
    ld a,$0A                    ; 023E 3E 0A      
    call GetNxRegA              ; 0240 CD DE 12   
    or $10                      ; 0243 F6 10        Enable DivMMC automap
    out (c),a                   ; 0245 ED 79        Write out the new port value
    call CopyPager              ; 0247 CD E3 00     Copy page switching routines to the RAM
    ld h,d                      ; 024A 62           HL := next byte after the paging trampolines
    ld l,e                      ; 024B 6B         
    ld (hl),c                   ; 024C 71           Clear OLDHL low byte (BC is zero after LDIR)
    inc de                      ; 024D 13           DE := $5B53
    ld bc,$015F                 ; 024E 01 5F 01     BC := 351 bytes
    ldir                        ; 0251 ED B0        Zero the $5B52-$5CB1 area
    ld hl,$5BFF                 ; 0253 21 FF 5B   
    ld (TMPSP),hl               ; 0256 22 6A 5B     Set TMPSP just below the sysvars area
    ld sp,hl                    ; 0259 F9           Set SP to $5BFF
    rst $18                     ; 025A DF           Call ROM1 routine $3471
    .defw $3471                 ; 025B 71 34        Initialize the stack
    ld a,$CF                    ; 025D 3E CF        A := RST $08 opcode
    ld ($5B5D),a                ; 025F 32 5D 5B     Set RAMRST for old-error reporting
    ld hl,$0040                 ; 0262 21 40 00     HL := warning buzz length
    ld ($5C38),hl               ; 0265 22 38 5C     Set RASP to $0040
    exx                         ; 0268 D9           Restore main registers
    ld a,c                      ; 0269 79           A := last available RAM bank
;
; The LDIR at $251 overwrote these variable values
;
    ld (LBANK),a                ; 026A 32 69 5B     Restore LBANK
    ld (UDG),hl                 ; 026D 22 7B 5C     Restore UDG
;
; --- Initialize NextOS, display system and BASIC workspace ---
;
    push bc                     ; 0270 C5           Save BC (B = 0 at this point)
    call L2329                  ; 0271 CD 29 23     Initialize NextOS structures
    rst $18                     ; 0274 DF           Call ROM1 routine
    .defw $14b9                 ; 0275 B9 14        ROM1: InitDisp - Initialize display system
    ld hl,$3C00                 ; 0277 21 00 3C     HL = $3C00 (CHARS - character set address)
    ld ($5C36),hl               ; 027A 22 36 5C     Store CHARS address
    set 4,(iy+$01)              ; 027D FD CB 01 E6  Set FLAGS bit 4 (K mode)
    ld a,$FF                    ; 0281 3E FF        A = $FF
    ld (iy),a                   ; 0283 FD 77 00     Set ERR_NR = $FF (no error)
    ld ($5B77),a                ; 0286 32 77 5B     Store $FF at $5B77
    ld ($5B78),a                ; 0289 32 78 5B     Store $FF at $5B78
    ld ($5B88),a                ; 028C 32 88 5B     Store $FF at $5B88
    ld ($5B89),a                ; 028F 32 89 5B     Store $FF at $5B89
    ld a,$54                    ; 0292 3E 54        A = $54
    ld ($5B79),a                ; 0294 32 79 5B     Store $54 at $5B79
    ld ($5B7A),a                ; 0297 32 7A 5B     Store $54 at $5B7A
    ld hl,$5CB6                 ; 029A 21 B6 5C     HL = $5CB6 (start of channel info area)
    ld ($5C4F),hl               ; 029D 22 4F 5C     Store in CHANS (channel information)
    ld de,$15AF                 ; 02A0 11 AF 15     DE = $15AF (source: channel data in ROM)
    ld bc,$0015                 ; 02A3 01 15 00     BC = $0015 (21 bytes to copy)
    ex de,hl                    ; 02A6 EB           Swap: HL = source, DE = dest
    rst $28                     ; 02A7 EF           Call ROM3 LDIR helper
    .defw $33C3                 ; 02A8 C3 33        Copy 21 bytes of channel data to RAM
    ex de,hl                    ; 02AA EB           HL = end of copied data ($5CB6 + 21)
    dec hl                      ; 02AB 2B           HL points to last byte of channel data
    ld (DATADD),hl              ; 02AC 22 57 5C     Store in DATADD (end of variables)
    inc hl                      ; 02AF 23           HL = start of BASIC program area
    ld (PROG),hl                ; 02B0 22 53 5C     Store in PROG (start of BASIC program)
    ld (VARS),hl                ; 02B3 22 4B 5C     Store in VARS (start of variables)
    ld (hl),$80                 ; 02B6 36 80        Write $80 (VARS end marker)
    inc hl                      ; 02B8 23           Advance pointer
    ld (E_LINE),hl              ; 02B9 22 59 5C     Store in E_LINE (edit line address)
    ld (hl),$0D                 ; 02BC 36 0D        Write $0D (ENTER character)
    inc hl                      ; 02BE 23           Advance pointer
    ld (hl),$80                 ; 02BF 36 80        Write $80 (end marker)
    inc hl                      ; 02C1 23           Advance pointer
    ld (WORKSP),hl              ; 02C2 22 61 5C     Store in WORKSP (workspace address)
    ld (STKBOT),hl              ; 02C5 22 63 5C     Store in STKBOT (stack bottom)
    ld (STKEND),hl              ; 02C8 22 65 5C     Store in STKEND (stack end)
    ld a,$38                    ; 02CB 3E 38        A = $38 (white INK, black PAPER)
    ld (ATTR_P),a               ; 02CD 32 8D 5C     Store in ATTR_P (permanent attributes)
    ld (ATTR_T),a               ; 02D0 32 8F 5C     Store in ATTR_T (temporary attributes)
    ld ($5B61),a                ; 02D3 32 61 5B     Store $38 at $5B61
    ld ($5B63),a                ; 02D6 32 63 5B     Store $38 at $5B63
    ld (BORDCR),a               ; 02D9 32 48 5C     Store in BORDCR (border color)
    ld a,$07                    ; 02DC 3E 07        A = $07 (white border)
    out ($FE),a                 ; 02DE D3 FE        Set border color via ULA port
    ld hl,$0523                 ; 02E0 21 23 05     HL = $0523 (REPDEL=$23, REPPER=$05)
    ld (REPDEL),hl              ; 02E3 22 09 5C     Set REPDEL and REPPER (key repeat timing)
    dec (iy-$3A)                ; 02E6 FD 35 C6     Decrement KSTATE+0 ($5C00): set to $FF (unused)
    dec (iy-$36)                ; 02E9 FD 35 CA     Decrement KSTATE+4 ($5C04): set to $FF (unused)
    ld hl,StrmIn                ; 02EC 21 C6 15     HL = $15C6 (source: stream data)
    ld de,STRMS                 ; 02EF 11 10 5C     DE = $5C10 (STRMS - streams data)
    ld bc,$000E                 ; 02F2 01 0E 00     BC = $000E (14 bytes to copy)
    rst $28                     ; 02F5 EF           Call ROM3 LDIR helper
    .defw $33C3                 ; 02F6 C3 33        Copy stream data to STRMS
    ld (iy+$31),$02             ; 02F8 FD 36 31 02  Set DF_SZ = $02 (display file size)
    ei                          ; 02FC FB           Enable interrupts
    rst $28                     ; 02FD EF           Call ROM3 routine
    .defw $0D6B                 ; 02FE 6B 0D        CLS48: 48K compatible CLS
    pop af                      ; 0300 F1           Recover saved BC bytes into AF
    rst $08                     ; 0301 CF           Enter ERROR-1 through the RST $08 vector
    push af                     ; 0302 F5           Save AF
    call CpyBits                ; 0303 CD 60 03     Call stripe pattern initialization
    rst $18                     ; 0306 DF           Call ROM1 routine
    .defw $1511                 ; 0307 11 15        Initialize Clip Windows and Display
    ld hl,$035A                 ; 0309 21 5A 03     HL := overlapping 6-byte display setup block
    ld de,DISP_MODE             ; 030C 11 B8 D5     DE := upper-RAM display setup buffer
    ld bc,$0006                 ; 030F 01 06 00     BC := 6 bytes
    ldir                        ; 0312 ED B0        Copy display setup block to upper RAM
    ex de,hl                    ; 0314 EB           HL = DISP_REC_END (after copy destination)
    scf                         ; 0315 37           Set carry flag (use nextreg read mode)
    call InitSprPat             ; 0316 CD 3C 0F     Initialize sprite patterns
    ld hl,$0020                 ; 0319 21 20 00     HL := sprite/display parameter $0020
    ld (DISP_PARAM0),hl         ; 031C 22 3D D7     Store $0020 at DISP_PARAM0
    inc l                       ; 031F 2C           L = $21
    inc l                       ; 0320 2C           L = $22
    inc l                       ; 0321 2C           L = $23
    ld (DISP_PARAM1),hl         ; 0322 22 3F D7     Store $0023 at DISP_PARAM1
    ld a,$03                    ; 0325 3E 03        A := $03
    ld ($5C81),a                ; 0327 32 81 5C     Set SCR_CT to $03
    rst $20                     ; 032A E7           Call ROM2 routine
    .defw $1E43                 ; 032B 43 1E        ROM2: Check core version and initialize
    ld hl,$10A5                 ; 032D 21 A5 10     HL := first 32-byte display-state template
    ld de,DISP_SLOT0            ; 0330 11 33 D6     DE := upper-RAM destination buffer
    ld bc,$3700                 ; 0333 01 00 37     BC := palette restore destination parameter
    call INIT_DISP_SLOT         ; 0336 CD D5 0F     Build primary display-state slot
    ld hl,$10C5                 ; 0339 21 C5 10     HL := second 32-byte display-state template
    ld de,DISP_SLOT1            ; 033C 11 DA D6     DE := upper-RAM destination buffer
    ld bc,$3760                 ; 033F 01 60 37     BC := palette restore destination parameter
INIT_ALT_SLOT
    call INIT_DISP_SLOT         ; 0342 CD D5 0F     Build alternate display-state slot
    call SAVE_DISP_STATE        ; 0345 CD 03 10     Save current display hardware state
    call INIT_SYS_FLAGS         ; 0348 CD 82 0F     Initialize ROM0 system flags
    pop af                      ; 034B F1           Restore AF (from PUSH AF at $0302)
    rst $18                     ; 034C DF           Call ROM1 routine $2BCA
    .defw $2BCA                 ; 034D CA 2B      
    set 2,(iy+$02)              ; 034F FD CB 02 50  Set TVFLAG bit 2
    xor $FD                     ; 0353 EE FD        Adjust returned mode/status byte
    ld (hl),$31                 ; 0355 36 31        Store ASCII "1" at ROM1-selected address
    ld (bc),a                   ; 0357 02           Store adjusted status byte
    jp RUN_BOOT_CMDS            ; 0358 C3 BE 11     Jump; high byte $11 is also DISP_MODE marker
; Initial display-state swap record copied from $035A to DISP_MODE. The block
; intentionally overlaps the high byte of the JP target above.
;   DISP_MODE = $11  display/mode marker
;   DISP_GMODE = $00  initial GMODE
;   DISP_L2SOFT = $00  initial L2SOFT / port $123B shadow
;   DISP_CPUSPEED = $03  initial CPU speed (NextReg $07 bits 1:0 = 28 MHz)
;   DISP_CHARS-DISP_CHARS_H = $3C00 initial CHARS value
;
    .defb $00                   ; 035B 00           DISP_GMODE: GMODE := $00
    .defb $00                   ; 035C 00           DISP_L2SOFT: L2SOFT/$123B := $00
    .defb $03                   ; 035D 03           DISP_CPUSPEED: CPU speed := $03
    .defb $00                   ; 035E 00           DISP_CHARS: CHARS low byte
    .defb $3C                   ; 035F 3C           DISP_CHARS_H: CHARS high byte
;
; Copy Bit Pattern Table to RAM
; Copies a 16-byte lookup table from ROM (StripePtr) to upper RAM ($D750).
; The table contains bit patterns used for graphics operations (bit masks).
; Entry: None
; Exit: 16 bytes copied to $D750
;
CpyBits
    ld hl,StripePtr             ; 0360 21 F7 0D     HL := StripePtr (source: bit pattern table)
    ld de,$D750                 ; 0363 11 50 D7     DE := $D750 (destination in upper RAM)
    ld bc,$0010                 ; 0366 01 10 00     BC := 16 bytes to copy
    ldir                        ; 0369 ED B0        Copy 16 bytes from ROM to RAM
    ret                         ; 036B C9           Return
L036C
    add hl,$0004                ; 036C ED 34 04 00
    ld b,a                      ; 0370 47         
    ld a,(hl)                   ; 0371 7E         
    cp $53                      ; 0372 FE 53      
    jr nz,L0391                 ; 0374 20 1B      
L0376
    push ix                     ; 0376 DD E5      
    ld a,(GMODE)                ; 0378 3A 7F 5C   
    and $0F                     ; 037B E6 0F      
    add a,$F2                   ; 037D C6 F2      
    ld xh,a                     ; 037F DD 67      
    ld xl,$00                   ; 0381 DD 2E 00   
    ld a,b                      ; 0384 78         
    call L2767                  ; 0385 CD 67 27   
    pop ix                      ; 0388 DD E1      
L038A
    ld hl,$15FE                 ; 038A 21 FE 15   
    push hl                     ; 038D E5         
L038E
    jp L5B48                    ; 038E C3 48 5B   
L0391
    cp $4B                      ; 0391 FE 4B      
    jr z,L0376                  ; 0393 28 E1      
    ld hl,($5C51)               ; 0395 2A 51 5C   
    ex de,hl                    ; 0398 EB         
    and a                       ; 0399 A7         
    sbc hl,de                   ; 039A ED 52      
    ld c,l                      ; 039C 4D         
    ex de,hl                    ; 039D EB         
    add hl,$0005                ; 039E ED 34 05 00
    cp $44                      ; 03A2 FE 44      
    jr z,L03C0                  ; 03A4 28 1A      
    dec c                       ; 03A6 0D         
    jr nz,L03C7                 ; 03A7 20 1E      
    ld e,(hl)                   ; 03A9 5E         
    inc hl                      ; 03AA 23         
    ld h,(hl)                   ; 03AB 66         
    ld l,e                      ; 03AC 6B         
    ld de,$15FE                 ; 03AD 11 FE 15   
    push de                     ; 03B0 D5         
    ld a,b                      ; 03B1 78         
    push ix                     ; 03B2 DD E5      
    ld ix,($5C51)               ; 03B4 DD 2A 51 5C
    call L03BF                  ; 03B8 CD BF 03   
    pop ix                      ; 03BB DD E1      
    jr L038E                    ; 03BD 18 CF      
L03BF
    jp (hl)                     ; 03BF E9         
L03C0
    ld a,c                      ; 03C0 79         
    dec a                       ; 03C1 3D         
    call L0481                  ; 03C2 CD 81 04   
    jr L038A                    ; 03C5 18 C3      
L03C7
    inc hl                      ; 03C7 23         
    inc hl                      ; 03C8 23         
    ld e,(hl)                   ; 03C9 5E         
    inc hl                      ; 03CA 23         
    ld h,(hl)                   ; 03CB 66         
    ld l,e                      ; 03CC 6B         
    ld de,$15FE                 ; 03CD 11 FE 15   
    push de                     ; 03D0 D5         
    push ix                     ; 03D1 DD E5      
    ld ix,($5C51)               ; 03D3 DD 2A 51 5C
    call L03BF                  ; 03D7 CD BF 03   
    pop ix                      ; 03DA DD E1      
    jr c,L038E                  ; 03DC 38 B0      
    jr z,L038E                  ; 03DE 28 AE      
L03E0
    ld a,$07                    ; 03E0 3E 07      
    jp L27B0                    ; 03E2 C3 B0 27   
L03E5
    ld hl,($5C51)               ; 03E5 2A 51 5C   
    push hl                     ; 03E8 E5         
    pop ix                      ; 03E9 DD E1      
    ld a,(hl)                   ; 03EB 7E         
    cp $4D                      ; 03EC FE 4D      
    jr nz,L0408                 ; 03EE 20 18      
    ld a,(ix+$01)               ; 03F0 DD 7E 01   
    cp $5B                      ; 03F3 FE 5B      
    jr nz,L0408                 ; 03F5 20 11      
    ld a,(ix+$04)               ; 03F7 DD 7E 04   
    cp $53                      ; 03FA FE 53      
    jr z,L042E                  ; 03FC 28 30      
    cp $4B                      ; 03FE FE 4B      
    jr z,L042E                  ; 0400 28 2C      
    cp $44                      ; 0402 FE 44      
    jr z,L045F                  ; 0404 28 59      
    jr L044A                    ; 0406 18 42      
L0408
    ld a,e                      ; 0408 7B         
    cp $04                      ; 0409 FE 04      
    jp z,L0495                  ; 040B CA 95 04   
    add hl,de                   ; 040E 19         
    ld c,(hl)                   ; 040F 4E         
    inc hl                      ; 0410 23         
    ld b,(hl)                   ; 0411 46

    exx                         ; 0418 D9

    call L0085                  ; 0420 CD 85 00   
L0423

    jr nz,L03E0                 ; 0424 20 BA

    ld d,b                      ; 0427 50         
    dec hl                      ; 0428 2B         
    ld de,$0002                 ; 0429 11 02 00

L042E
    ld a,(GMODE)                ; 042E 3A 7F 5C   
    and $0F                     ; 0431 E6 0F      
    add a,$F2                   ; 0433 C6 F2

    pop de                      ; 0478 D1

L047B

L0481
    ld c,(hl)                   ; 0481 4E
