    rst $10                     ; 0E42 D7         
    djnz L0E39                  ; 0E43 10 F4      
    ld a,(de)                   ; 0E45 1A         
    ld (ATTR_T),a               ; 0E46 32 8F 5C   
    ld a,$20                    ; 0E49 3E 20      
    rst $10                     ; 0E4B D7         
    pop de                      ; 0E4C D1         
    jp L0E58                    ; 0E4D C3 58 0E   
L0E50
    ld de,StripePtr             ; 0E50 11 F7 0D   
L0E53
    jr L0E58                    ; 0E53 18 03      
L0E55
    ld de,$D750                 ; 0E55 11 50 D7   
L0E58
    ld hl,(UDG)                 ; 0E58 2A 7B 5C   
    ld (UDG),de                 ; 0E5B ED 53 7B 5C
    ret                         ; 0E5F C9         
    pop hl                      ; 0E60 E1         
    pop hl                      ; 0E61 E1         
    ei                          ; 0E62 FB         
    halt                        ; 0E63 76         
    res 5,(iy+$01)              ; 0E64 FD CB 01 AE
    rst $08                     ; 0E68 CF         
    ld a,$02                    ; 0E69 3E 02      
    rst $28                     ; 0E6B EF         
    ld bc,$CD16                 ; 0E6C 01 16 CD   
    add a,d                     ; 0E6F 82         
    rrca                        ; 0E70 0F         
    ld a,(DISP_MODE)            ; 0E71 3A B8 D5   
    cp $FE                      ; 0E74 FE FE      
    push af                     ; 0E76 F5         
    cp $04                      ; 0E77 FE 04      
    jr c,L0E80                  ; 0E79 38 05      
    ld a,($5C3A)                ; 0E7B 3A 3A 5C   
    cp $FF                      ; 0E7E FE FF      
L0E80
    call c,WAIT_KEY             ; 0E80 DC E0 11   
    call SAVE_DISP_STATE        ; 0E83 CD 03 10   
    pop af                      ; 0E86 F1         
    jp z,L2C1F                  ; 0E87 CA 1F 2C   
    jp nc,CLS_MAIN_AREA         ; 0E8A D2 B8 11   
    call ALTROM_CALL            ; 0E8D CD 68 00     Call AltROM routine addressed inline below
    .defw $02E5                 ; 0E90 E5 02        AltROM routine $02E5
    call L0EDD                  ; 0E92 CD DD 0E   
    xor a                       ; 0E95 AF         
    rst $18                     ; 0E96 DF         
    cp c                        ; 0E97 B9         
    inc d                       ; 0E98 14         
    ret                         ; 0E99 C9         
    ld a,(DISP_PARAM0)          ; 0E9A 3A 3D D7   
    sub $20                     ; 0E9D D6 20      
    jr z,L0EA3                  ; 0E9F 28 02      
    ld a,$09                    ; 0EA1 3E 09      
L0EA3
    push af                     ; 0EA3 F5         
    rst $18                     ; 0EA4 DF         
    xor c                       ; 0EA5 A9         
    inc d                       ; 0EA6 14         
    pop af                      ; 0EA7 F1         
    ret z                       ; 0EA8 C8         
    push ix                     ; 0EA9 DD E5      
    ld ix,$FB00                 ; 0EAB DD 21 00 FB
    ld hl,$0EC6                 ; 0EAF 21 C6 0E   
    call L2727                  ; 0EB2 CD 27 27   
    ld a,(DISP_PARAM0)          ; 0EB5 3A 3D D7   
    cp $40                      ; 0EB8 FE 40      
    ld a,$08                    ; 0EBA 3E 08      
    jr z,L0EC0                  ; 0EBC 28 02      
    ld a,$06                    ; 0EBE 3E 06      
L0EC0
    call L275B                  ; 0EC0 CD 5B 27   
    pop ix                      ; 0EC3 DD E1      
    ret                         ; 0EC5 C9         
    ld a,(de)                   ; 0EC6 1A         
    rla                         ; 0EC7 17         
    sbc a,(hl)                  ; 0EC8 9E         
L0EC9
    ld a,(GMODE)                ; 0EC9 3A 7F 5C   
    and $0F                     ; 0ECC E6 0F      
    jr z,L0ED5                  ; 0ECE 28 05      
    res 5,(iy+$02)              ; 0ED0 FD CB 02 AE
    ret                         ; 0ED4 C9         
L0ED5
    ld (iy+$31),$02             ; 0ED5 FD 36 31 02
    rst $28                     ; 0ED9 EF         
    ld l,(hl)                   ; 0EDA 6E         
    dec c                       ; 0EDB 0D         
    ret                         ; 0EDC C9         
L0EDD
    ld a,(DISP_MODE)            ; 0EDD 3A B8 D5   
    cp $04                      ; 0EE0 FE 04      
    ret z                       ; 0EE2 C8         
    cp $08                      ; 0EE3 FE 08      
    call nz,L1073               ; 0EE5 C4 73 10   
L0EE8
    ld a,($5B7B)                ; 0EE8 3A 7B 5B     Save current L2SOFT / port $123B shadow
    push af                     ; 0EEB F5         
    ld a,(GMODE)                ; 0EEC 3A 7F 5C     Save current GMODE
    push af                     ; 0EEF F5         
    halt                        ; 0EF0 76           Wait for display-safe timing
    xor a                       ; 0EF1 AF           Select Timex/ULA mode 0 while swapping
    out ($FF),a                 ; 0EF2 D3 FF      
    ld hl,DISP_GMODE            ; 0EF4 21 B9 D5     HL := display-state swap record
    ld a,(hl)                   ; 0EF7 7E           A := new GMODE from DISP_GMODE
    push hl                     ; 0EF8 E5         
    rst $18                     ; 0EF9 DF           Call ROM1 graphics-mode setter $14A9
    .defw $14A9                 ; 0EFA A9 14      
    pop hl                      ; 0EFC E1         
    pop af                      ; 0EFD F1           A := previous GMODE
    ld (hl),a                   ; 0EFE 77           Store previous GMODE back to DISP_GMODE
    inc hl                      ; 0EFF 23           HL -> DISP_L2SOFT
    ld a,(hl)                   ; 0F00 7E           A := new L2SOFT / port $123B value
    ld bc,$123B                 ; 0F01 01 3B 12     Layer 2 control port
    ld ($5B7B),a                ; 0F04 32 7B 5B     Update L2SOFT shadow
    out (c),a                   ; 0F07 ED 79        Apply Layer 2 control value
    pop af                      ; 0F09 F1           A := previous L2SOFT value
    ld (hl),a                   ; 0F0A 77           Store previous L2SOFT back to DISP_L2SOFT
    inc hl                      ; 0F0B 23           HL -> DISP_CPUSPEED
    call L3DB1                  ; 0F0C CD B1 3D     A := current CPU speed bits
    ld e,(hl)                   ; 0F0F 5E           E := new CPU speed bits from DISP_CPUSPEED
    ld (hl),a                   ; 0F10 77           Store previous CPU speed back to DISP_CPUSPEED
    out (c),e                   ; 0F11 ED 59        Apply new CPU speed through NextReg data port
    inc hl                      ; 0F13 23           HL -> DISP_CHARS
    ld bc,($5C36)               ; 0F14 ED 4B 36 5C  BC := current CHARS
    ld e,(hl)                   ; 0F18 5E           E := new CHARS low byte
    ld (hl),c                   ; 0F19 71           Store previous CHARS low byte to DISP_CHARS
    inc hl                      ; 0F1A 23           HL -> DISP_CHARS_H
    ld d,(hl)                   ; 0F1B 56           D := new CHARS high byte
    ld (hl),b                   ; 0F1C 70           Store previous CHARS high byte to DISP_CHARS_H
    inc hl                      ; 0F1D 23         
    ld ($5C36),de               ; 0F1E ED 53 36 5C  Apply new CHARS
    call L0F3B                  ; 0F22 CD 3B 0F   
    ld a,(DISP_MODE)            ; 0F25 3A B8 D5   
    cp $08                      ; 0F28 FE 08      
    ret z                       ; 0F2A C8         
    call L2708                  ; 0F2B CD 08 27   
    ld h,$F7                    ; 0F2E 26 F7      
    call L0F5F                  ; 0F30 CD 5F 0F   
    ld h,$FB                    ; 0F33 26 FB      
    call L0F5F                  ; 0F35 CD 5F 0F   
    jp L2797                    ; 0F38 C3 97 27   
L0F3B
    and a                       ; 0F3B A7         
;
; Initialize Sprite Patterns
; Writes sprite pattern data from HL to sprite pattern memory.
; Uses nextreg $1C (Clip Window) and double nested loop.
; Entry: HL = pointer to sprite pattern data (or after EX DE,HL and CF)
; Exit: Sprite patterns initialized
;
InitSprPat
    ld de,$0418                 ; 0F3C 11 18 04     D = $04 (outer loop: 4 iterations), E = $18 (pattern index)
    nextreg $1C,$0F             ; 0F3F ED 91 1C 0F  Set nextreg $1C (Clip Window Tilemap) = $0F
`InitSpr1
    push de                     ; 0F43 D5           Save DE (outer loop counter and pattern index)
    ld b,$04                    ; 0F44 06 04        B = $04 (inner loop: 4 iterations)
`InitSpr2
    push bc                     ; 0F46 C5           Save BC (inner loop counter)
    call WSprPat                ; 0F47 CD 70 0F     Call sprite pattern write routine
    dec e                       ; 0F4A 1D           E = E - 1 (decrement pattern index)
    pop bc                      ; 0F4B C1           Restore BC (inner loop counter)
    djnz `InitSpr2              ; 0F4C 10 F8        Loop 4 times (inner loop)
    pop de                      ; 0F4E D1           Restore DE (outer loop counter)
    inc e                       ; 0F4F 1C           E = E + 1 (adjust pattern index)
    dec d                       ; 0F50 15           D = D - 1 (decrement outer loop counter)
    jr nz,`InitSpr1             ; 0F51 20 F0        Loop 4 times (outer loop)
    ret                         ; 0F53 C9           Return
;
;
;
L0F54
    ld d,h                      ; 0F54 54         
    ld a,l                      ; 0F55 7D         
    ld c,$30                    ; 0F56 0E 30      
    xor c                       ; 0F58 A9         
    ld e,a                      ; 0F59 5F         
    ld b,$00                    ; 0F5A 06 00      
    ldir                        ; 0F5C ED B0      
    ret                         ; 0F5E C9         
L0F5F
    ld l,$00                    ; 0F5F 2E 00      
    ld d,h                      ; 0F61 54         
    ld e,$30                    ; 0F62 1E 30      
    ld b,e                      ; 0F64 43         
L0F65
    ld c,(hl)                   ; 0F65 4E         
    ld a,(de)                   ; 0F66 1A         
    ld (hl),a                   ; 0F67 77         
    ld a,c                      ; 0F68 79         
    ld (de),a                   ; 0F69 12         
    inc hl                      ; 0F6A 23         
    inc de                      ; 0F6B 13         
    djnz L0F65                  ; 0F6C 10 F7      
    ret                         ; 0F6E C9         
    and a                       ; 0F6F A7         
;
; Write Sprite Pattern Byte
; Writes a single byte to sprite pattern memory via nextreg interface.
; Uses carry flag to determine whether to use data from HL or read from nextreg.
; Entry: HL = pointer to source data, E = sprite pattern index, Carry flag
; Exit: HL incremented, E incremented, D = value read from nextreg
;
WSprPat
    ld a,(hl)                   ; 0F70 7E           A = byte from source data
    ld bc,$243B                 ; 0F71 01 3B 24     BC = $243B (Nextreg select port)
    out (c),e                   ; 0F74 ED 59        Select sprite pattern register E
    inc b                       ; 0F76 04           BC = $253B (Nextreg data port)
    in d,(c)                    ; 0F77 ED 50        D = read current value from nextreg
    jr nc,`WSpr1                ; 0F79 30 01        If NC (carry clear), jump to write A
    ld a,d                      ; 0F7B 7A           If C (carry set), A = D (use read value)
`WSpr1
    out (c),a                   ; 0F7C ED 79        Write A to nextreg data port
    ld (hl),d                   ; 0F7E 72           Store D back to (HL)
    inc hl                      ; 0F7F 23           HL = HL + 1
    inc e                       ; 0F80 1C           E = E + 1 (next pattern index)
    ret                         ; 0F81 C9           Return
;
; Initialize ROM0 system flags.
; Clears channel/display flags and sets FLAGS bits used by the keyboard/channel
; startup state.
; Entry: None.
; Exit: CDFLAG and FLAGS-related startup state configured.
;
INIT_SYS_FLAGS
    xor a                       ; 0F82 AF           A = $00
    ld ($5C41),a                ; 0F83 32 41 5C     CDFLAG = $00 (clear channel/display flags)
    ld (iy-$30),$02             ; 0F86 FD 36 D0 02  Set ($5C0A) = $02 (STRMS+26 offset)
    ld hl,$5C3B                 ; 0F8A 21 3B 5C     HL = $5C3B (FLAGS system variable)
    ld a,(hl)                   ; 0F8D 7E           A = current FLAGS value
    or $0C                      ; 0F8E F6 0C        Set bits 2-3 (keyboard mode, K channel)
    ld (hl),a                   ; 0F90 77           Store back to FLAGS
    ret                         ; 0F91 C9           Return
;
; Read Palette Data to Memory
; Reads all 32 palette entries (64 bytes) from hardware palette to memory at HL.
; Each palette entry is 2 bytes (9-bit color: RRRGGGBBB).
; Entry: HL = destination address for palette data
; Exit: HL = HL + 64, palette data saved
;
L0F92
    xor a                       ; 0F92 AF           A = $00
    ld d,$20                    ; 0F93 16 20        D = $20 (32 palette entries)
    ld bc,$243B                 ; 0F95 01 3B 24     BC = $243B (Nextreg select port)
    nextreg $43,a               ; 0F98 ED 92 43     Nextreg $43 = $00 (palette control: standard palette)
    ld e,$00                    ; 0F9B 1E 00        E = $00 (palette index)
L0F9D
    ld a,e                      ; 0F9D 7B           A = E (current palette index)
    nextreg $40,a               ; 0F9E ED 92 40     Nextreg $40 = palette index
    ld a,$41                    ; 0FA1 3E 41        A = $41 (palette value register)
    out (c),a                   ; 0FA3 ED 79        Select nextreg $41
    inc b                       ; 0FA5 04           BC = $253B (Nextreg data port)
    in a,(c)                    ; 0FA6 ED 78        A = read first byte of palette entry
    dec b                       ; 0FA8 05           BC = $243B (Nextreg select port)
    ld (hl),a                   ; 0FA9 77           Save first byte to (HL)
    inc hl                      ; 0FAA 23           HL = HL + 1
    ld a,$44                    ; 0FAB 3E 44        A = $44 (enhanced palette register)
    out (c),a                   ; 0FAD ED 79        Select nextreg $44
    inc b                       ; 0FAF 04           BC = $253B (Nextreg data port)
    in a,(c)                    ; 0FB0 ED 78        A = read second byte of palette entry
    dec b                       ; 0FB2 05           BC = $243B (Nextreg select port)
    ld (hl),a                   ; 0FB3 77           Save second byte to (HL)
    inc hl                      ; 0FB4 23           HL = HL + 1
    inc e                       ; 0FB5 1C           E = E + 1 (next palette index)
    dec d                       ; 0FB6 15           D = D - 1 (decrement counter)
    jr nz,L0F9D                 ; 0FB7 20 E4        Loop for all 32 entries
    ret                         ; 0FB9 C9           Return
L0FBA
    xor a                       ; 0FBA AF         
    ld d,$20                    ; 0FBB 16 20      
    ld e,$00                    ; 0FBD 1E 00      
    nextreg $43,a               ; 0FBF ED 92 43   
L0FC2
    ld a,e                      ; 0FC2 7B         
    nextreg $40,a               ; 0FC3 ED 92 40   
    ld a,(hl)                   ; 0FC6 7E         
    inc hl                      ; 0FC7 23         
    nextreg $44,a               ; 0FC8 ED 92 44   
    ld a,(hl)                   ; 0FCB 7E         
    inc hl                      ; 0FCC 23         
    nextreg $44,a               ; 0FCD ED 92 44   
    inc e                       ; 0FD0 1C         
    dec d                       ; 0FD1 15         
    jr nz,L0FC2                 ; 0FD2 20 EE      
    ret                         ; 0FD4 C9         
;
; Initialize a display-state slot from a template.
; Copies a 32-byte display-state template from HL to DE, appends the current
; standard palette to that slot, then calls the ROM2 palette helper for the
; 96-byte record at DE using the original BC value as the palette destination.
; Entry: HL = 32-byte template, DE = display-state slot, BC = palette destination.
; Exit: Carry set after ROM2 palette helper returns.
;
INIT_DISP_SLOT
    push bc                     ; 0FD5 C5           Save BC (parameter)
    ld bc,$0020                 ; 0FD6 01 20 00     BC = $0020 (32 bytes)
    push de                     ; 0FD9 D5           Save DE (destination)
    ldir                        ; 0FDA ED B0        Copy 32 bytes from HL to DE
    ex de,hl                    ; 0FDC EB           HL = end of destination
    call L0F92                  ; 0FDD CD 92 0F     Save palette data to HL
    pop hl                      ; 0FE0 E1           HL = original DE (start of copied data)
    pop de                      ; 0FE1 D1           DE = original BC parameter
    jr L0FFB                    ; 0FE2 18 17        Jump to continue
;
; Palette Restoration Dispatcher
; Selects destination address based on A value and calls ROM2 palette routine.
; Entry: A = mode selector (1-5)
; Exit: Palette restored to hardware, carry set
;
L0FE4
    ld de,$3700                 ; 0FE4 11 00 37     DE = $3700 (default destination)
    dec a                       ; 0FE7 3D           A = A - 1
    jr z,L0FFA                  ; 0FE8 28 10        If A was 1, exchange DE/HL
    dec a                       ; 0FEA 3D           A = A - 1
    jr z,L0FFB                  ; 0FEB 28 0E        If A was 2, use $3700
    dec a                       ; 0FED 3D           A = A - 1
    jr z,L1014                  ; 0FEE 28 24        If A was 3, jump to L1014
    dec a                       ; 0FF0 3D           A = A - 1
    jp z,L1078                  ; 0FF1 CA 78 10     If A was 4, jump to L1078
    ld de,$3760                 ; 0FF4 11 60 37     DE = $3760 (alternate destination)
    dec a                       ; 0FF7 3D           A = A - 1
    jr nz,L0FFB                 ; 0FF8 20 01        If A was not 5, continue
L0FFA
    ex de,hl                    ; 0FFA EB           Swap HL and DE
L0FFB
    ld bc,$0060                 ; 0FFB 01 60 00     BC = $0060 (96 bytes)
    rst $20                     ; 0FFE E7           Call ROM2 routine
    .defw $0068                 ; 0FFF 68 00        ROM2: Restore palette (96 bytes at $3700/$3760)
    scf                         ; 1001 37           Set carry flag
    ret                         ; 1002 C9           Return
;
; Save the current display hardware state.
; Stores NextReg display controls, Spectrum attributes, border state, and
; palette data into the active display-state slot. The selected slot depends on
; the display mode marker at DISP_MODE.
; Entry: None.
; Exit: Display-state slot updated and carry set, unless mode $04 skips the save.
;
SAVE_DISP_STATE
    ld a,(DISP_MODE)            ; 1003 3A B8 D5     A = display mode indicator
    cp $04                      ; 1006 FE 04        Compare with $04
    ret z                       ; 1008 C8           If mode 4, return (no save needed)
    call L0EE8                  ; 1009 CD E8 0E     Save and restore display settings
    ld hl,DISP_SAVE0            ; 100C 21 94 D6     HL = DISP_SAVE0 (default buffer address)
    ld de,$3760                 ; 100F 11 60 37     DE = $3760 (palette destination)
    jr L101A                    ; 1012 18 06        Jump to save routine
L1014
    ld hl,DISP_SAVE1            ; 1014 21 ED D5     HL = DISP_SAVE1 (alternate buffer address)
    ld de,$3700                 ; 1017 11 00 37     DE = $3700 (alternate palette destination)
L101A
    push de                     ; 101A D5           Save DE (palette destination)
    ld a,$43                    ; 101B 3E 43        A = $43 (palette control nextreg)
    call GetNxRegA              ; 101D CD DE 12     Read nextreg $43
    ld (hl),a                   ; 1020 77           Save to buffer
    inc hl                      ; 1021 23           HL = HL + 1
    ld a,$15                    ; 1022 3E 15        A = $15 (sprite/layer control)
    call GetNxRegA              ; 1024 CD DE 12     Read nextreg $15
    ld (hl),a                   ; 1027 77           Save to buffer
    inc hl                      ; 1028 23           HL = HL + 1
    ld a,$14                    ; 1029 3E 14        A = $14 (transparency control)
    call GetNxRegA              ; 102B CD DE 12     Read nextreg $14
    ld (hl),a                   ; 102E 77           Save to buffer
    inc hl                      ; 102F 23           HL = HL + 1
    ld a,(ATTR_P)               ; 1030 3A 8D 5C     A = permanent attributes
    ld (hl),a                   ; 1033 77           Save to buffer
    inc hl                      ; 1034 23           HL = HL + 1
    ld a,($5B62)                ; 1035 3A 62 5B     A = system variable
    ld (hl),a                   ; 1038 77           Save to buffer
    inc hl                      ; 1039 23           HL = HL + 1
    ld a,(BORDCR)               ; 103A 3A 48 5C     A = border color
    ld (hl),a                   ; 103D 77           Save to buffer
    inc hl                      ; 103E 23           HL = HL + 1
    call L0F92                  ; 103F CD 92 0F     Save palette data (64 bytes) to HL
    pop de                      ; 1042 D1           DE = palette destination
    push hl                     ; 1043 E5           Save HL (end of buffer)
    call L0FFA                  ; 1044 CD FA 0F     Restore palette from buffer to hardware
    pop hl                      ; 1047 E1           Restore HL
    push hl                     ; 1048 E5           Save HL again
    add hl,$0020                ; 1049 ED 34 20 00  HL = HL + $20 (skip 32 bytes)
    call L0FBA                  ; 104D CD BA 0F     Write palette data from HL
    pop hl                      ; 1050 E1           Restore HL
    ld a,(hl)                   ; 1051 7E           A = first saved byte (ATTR_P)
    ld (ATTR_P),a               ; 1052 32 8D 5C     Restore ATTR_P
    ld (BORDCR),a               ; 1055 32 48 5C     Set BORDCR = ATTR_P
    push af                     ; 1058 F5           Save AF
    rra                         ; 1059 1F           Rotate right (extract border bits)
    rra                         ; 105A 1F         
    rra                         ; 105B 1F         
    out ($FE),a                 ; 105C D3 FE        Set hardware border color
    pop af                      ; 105E F1           Restore AF
    cpl                         ; 105F 2F           Complement A
    and $38                     ; 1060 E6 38        Mask bits 3-5
    ld ($5B62),a                ; 1062 32 62 5B     Save to $5B62
    ld a,$4A                    ; 1065 3E 4A        A = $4A (nextreg: transparency fallback)
    call GetNxRegA              ; 1067 CD DE 12     Read nextreg $4A
    nextreg $14,a               ; 106A ED 92 14     Write to nextreg $14
    nextreg $15,$00             ; 106D ED 91 15 00  Clear nextreg $15
    scf                         ; 1071 37           Set carry flag
    ret                         ; 1072 C9           Return
L1073
    ld hl,DISP_SAVE0            ; 1073 21 94 D6   
    jr L107B                    ; 1076 18 03      
L1078
    ld hl,DISP_SAVE1            ; 1078 21 ED D5   
L107B
    ld a,(hl)                   ; 107B 7E         
    push af                     ; 107C F5         
    inc hl                      ; 107D 23         
    ld a,(hl)                   ; 107E 7E         
    nextreg $15,a               ; 107F ED 92 15   
    inc hl                      ; 1082 23         
    ld a,(hl)                   ; 1083 7E         
    nextreg $14,a               ; 1084 ED 92 14   
    inc hl                      ; 1087 23         
    ld a,(hl)                   ; 1088 7E         
    ld (ATTR_P),a               ; 1089 32 8D 5C   
    inc hl                      ; 108C 23         
    ld a,(hl)                   ; 108D 7E         
    ld ($5B62),a                ; 108E 32 62 5B   
    inc hl                      ; 1091 23         
    ld a,(hl)                   ; 1092 7E         
    ld (BORDCR),a               ; 1093 32 48 5C   
    rra                         ; 1096 1F         
    rra                         ; 1097 1F         
    rra                         ; 1098 1F         
    out ($FE),a                 ; 1099 D3 FE      
    inc hl                      ; 109B 23         
    call L0FBA                  ; 109C CD BA 0F   
    pop af                      ; 109F F1         
    nextreg $43,a               ; 10A0 ED 92 43   
    scf                         ; 10A3 37         
    ret                         ; 10A4 C9         
;
; Palette Data Table 1 (32 bytes)
; Default palette configuration copied to DISP_SLOT0 during initialization
;
    .defb $38,$50,$56,$66       ; 10A5 38 50 56 66  Palette entries 0-1
    .defb $65,$45,$47,$07       ; 10A9 65 45 47 07  Palette entries 2-3
    .defb $30,$28,$28,$28       ; 10AD 30 28 28 28  Palette entries 4-5
    .defb $28,$00,$00,$00       ; 10B1 28 00 00 00  Palette entries 6-7
    .defb $38,$38,$38,$38       ; 10B5 38 38 38 38  Palette entries 8-9
    .defb $38,$00,$00,$00       ; 10B9 38 00 00 00  Palette entries 10-11
    .defb $00,$00,$00,$00       ; 10BD 00 00 00 00  Palette entries 12-13
    .defb $00,$00,$00,$00       ; 10C1 00 00 00 00  Palette entries 14-15
;
; Palette Data Table 2 (32 bytes)
; Alternate palette configuration copied to DISP_SLOT1 during initialization
;
    .defb $38,$50,$56,$66       ; 10C5 38 50 56 66  Palette entries 0-1
    .defb $65,$45,$47,$07       ; 10C9 65 45 47 07  Palette entries 2-3
    .defb $68,$78,$69,$79       ; 10CD 68 78 69 79  Palette entries 4-5
    .defb $29,$39,$CF,$E7       ; 10D1 29 39 CF E7  Palette entries 6-7
    .defb $DF,$EF,$D7,$3D       ; 10D5 DF EF D7 3D  Palette entries 8-9
    .defb $38,$3B,$3C,$3A       ; 10D9 38 3B 3C 3A  Palette entries 10-11
    .defb $39,$3D,$38,$38       ; 10DD 39 3D 38 38  Palette entries 12-13
    .defb $00,$00,$00,$00       ; 10E1 00 00 00 00  Palette entries 14-15
L10E5
    nextreg $51,$10             ; 10E5 ED 91 51 10
    ld hl,$23B7                 ; 10E9 21 B7 23   
    scf                         ; 10EC 37         
    call InitSprPat             ; 10ED CD 3C 0F   
    xor a                       ; 10F0 AF         
L10F1
    out ($FE),a                 ; 10F1 D3 FE      
    ld bc,$243B                 ; 10F3 01 3B 24   
    ld a,$15                    ; 10F6 3E 15      
    out (c),a                   ; 10F8 ED 79      
    inc b                       ; 10FA 04         
    in h,(c)                    ; 10FB ED 60      
    in a,($FF)                  ; 10FD DB FF      
    ld l,a                      ; 10FF 6F         
    push hl                     ; 1100 E5         
    or $38                      ; 1101 F6 38      
    out ($FF),a                 ; 1103 D3 FF      
    res 1,h                     ; 1105 CB 8C      
    out (c),h                   ; 1107 ED 61      
    ld hl,($5C78)               ; 1109 2A 78 5C   
    res 7,h                     ; 110C CB BC      
    res 7,l                     ; 110E CB BD      
    ld a,l                      ; 1110 7D         
    and $03                     ; 1111 E6 03      
    inc a                       ; 1113 3C         
    ld d,a                      ; 1114 57         
    ld a,h                      ; 1115 7C         
L1116
    and $03                     ; 1116 E6 03      
    inc a                       ; 1118 3C         
L1119
    ld e,a                      ; 1119 5F         
L111A
    ld a,h                      ; 111A 7C         
    add a,d                     ; 111B 82         
    cp $E8                      ; 111C FE E8      
    jr c,L1125                  ; 111E 38 05      
    ld a,d                      ; 1120 7A         
    neg                         ; 1121 ED 44      
    ld d,a                      ; 1123 57         
    add a,h                     ; 1124 84         
L1125
    ld h,a                      ; 1125 67         
    ld c,$C8                    ; 1126 0E C8      
    call L1172                  ; 1128 CD 72 11   
    ld a,l                      ; 112B 7D         
    add a,e                     ; 112C 83         
    cp $A8                      ; 112D FE A8      
    jr c,L1136                  ; 112F 38 05      
    ld a,e                      ; 1131 7B         
    neg                         ; 1132 ED 44      
    ld e,a                      ; 1134 5F         
    add a,l                     ; 1135 85         
L1136
    ld l,a                      ; 1136 6F         
    ld c,$50                    ; 1137 0E 50      
    call L1172                  ; 1139 CD 72 11   
    halt                        ; 113C 76         
    bit 5,(iy+$01)              ; 113D FD CB 01 6E
    jr z,L111A                  ; 1141 28 D7      
    xor a                       ; 1143 AF         
    ld bc,$FF90                 ; 1144 01 90 FF   
    call L117C                  ; 1147 CD 7C 11   
    xor a                       ; 114A AF         
    ld bc,$BF20                 ; 114B 01 20 BF   
    call L117C                  ; 114E CD 7C 11   
    pop hl                      ; 1151 E1         
    ld a,h                      ; 1152 7C         
    nextreg $15,a               ; 1153 ED 92 15   
    ld a,l                      ; 1156 7D         
    out ($FF),a                 ; 1157 D3 FF      
    ld a,(BORDCR)               ; 1159 3A 48 5C   
    rrca                        ; 115C 0F         
    rrca                        ; 115D 0F         
    rrca                        ; 115E 0F         
    out ($FE),a                 ; 115F D3 FE      
    res 5,(iy+$01)              ; 1161 FD CB 01 AE
    ld hl,$23B7                 ; 1165 21 B7 23   
    call L0F3B                  ; 1168 CD 3B 0F   
    nextreg $51,$FF             ; 116B ED 91 51 FF
    jp WAIT_KEY                 ; 116F C3 E0 11   
L1172
    ld b,$18                    ; 1172 06 18      
    bit 7,(iy+$47)              ; 1174 FD CB 47 7E
    jr z,L117C                  ; 1178 28 02      
    ld b,$FF                    ; 117A 06 FF      
L117C
    push hl                     ; 117C E5         
    push de                     ; 117D D5         
    push bc                     ; 117E C5         
    ld e,a                      ; 117F 5F         
    add a,b                     ; 1180 80         
    ld d,a                      ; 1181 57         
    ld hl,$0318                 ; 1182 21 18 03   
L1185
    ld bc,$243B                 ; 1185 01 3B 24   
    out (c),l                   ; 1188 ED 69      
    inc b                       ; 118A 04         
    out (c),e                   ; 118B ED 59      
    out (c),d                   ; 118D ED 51      
    inc l                       ; 118F 2C         
    dec h                       ; 1190 25         
    jr nz,L1185                 ; 1191 20 F2      
    dec b                       ; 1193 05         
    out (c),l                   ; 1194 ED 69      
    inc b                       ; 1196 04         
    pop hl                      ; 1197 E1         
    sla l                       ; 1198 CB 25      
    push af                     ; 119A F5         
    ld a,e                      ; 119B 7B         
    jr nc,L11A0                 ; 119C 30 02      
    srl a                       ; 119E CB 3F      
L11A0
    bit 7,l                     ; 11A0 CB 7D      
    res 7,l                     ; 11A2 CB BD      
    jr z,L11A7                  ; 11A4 28 01      
    add a,l                     ; 11A6 85         
L11A7
    out (c),a                   ; 11A7 ED 79      
    pop af                      ; 11A9 F1         
    ld a,d                      ; 11AA 7A         
    jr nc,L11AF                 ; 11AB 30 02      
    srl a                       ; 11AD CB 3F      
L11AF
    add a,l                     ; 11AF 85         
    out (c),a                   ; 11B0 ED 79      
    pop de                      ; 11B2 D1         
    pop hl                      ; 11B3 E1         
    ret                         ; 11B4 C9         
    call SAVE_DISP_STATE        ; 11B5 CD 03 10   
CLS_MAIN_AREA
    ld bc,$0017                 ; 11B8 01 17 00   
    call CLS_LINES              ; 11BB CD 77 12   
;
; Startup command runner.
; Set the display-mode marker to $FF, reset the stack, then run the startup
; command scan twice: first with selector 0, then with selector 1.
; RUN_BOOT_MENU leaves MENU_SECTION non-zero while a matching startup command remains.
;
RUN_BOOT_CMDS
    ld a,$FF                    ; 11BE 3E FF        Mark startup display mode as unselected
    ld (DISP_MODE),a            ; 11C0 32 B8 D5     Store display-mode marker
    ld sp,$5BFF                 ; 11C3 31 FF 5B     Reset stack below system variables
`BOOT_CMD_ZERO
    xor a                       ; 11C6 AF           A := startup command selector 0
`BOOT_CMD_SCAN
    call RUN_BOOT_MENU          ; 11C7 CD 45 06     Scan/run matching startup command
    ld a,(MENU_SECTION)         ; 11CA 3A 44 D7     Read remaining command marker
    and a                       ; 11CD A7           Any command still pending?
    jr nz,`BOOT_CMD_ZERO        ; 11CE 20 F6        Yes: restart with selector 0
    ld a,$01                    ; 11D0 3E 01        A := startup command selector 1
    jr `BOOT_CMD_SCAN           ; 11D2 18 F3        Keep scanning selector 1 forever
;
; External/indirect display-mode handoff.
; If the display-mode marker is still zero, discard this routine's caller return
; and call AltROM routine $1332. ALTROM_CALL consumes the following word as its
; inline target operand; the byte after that word is the ROM0 continuation.
;
    ld a,(DISP_MODE)            ; 11D4 3A B8 D5   
    and a                       ; 11D7 A7         
    ret nz                      ; 11D8 C0         
    pop hl                      ; 11D9 E1           Drop caller return; AltROM path tail-exits
    call ALTROM_CALL            ; 11DA CD 68 00     Call AltROM routine addressed inline below
    .defw $1332                 ; 11DD 32 13        AltROM display-mode setup routine
    ret                         ; 11DF C9           Return after AltROM disables itself
;
; Wait for a key and handle Spectrum input-mode toggle keys.
; Entry: A optionally overrides the SCR_CT countdown. The normal entry reads
; SCR_CT, masks off bit 7, and then waits for FLAGS bit 5 to signal LAST_K.
;
WAIT_KEY
    ld a,($5C81)                ; 11E0 3A 81 5C     A := SCR_CT
    and $7F                     ; 11E3 E6 7F        Ignore the high flag bit
`WAIT_KEY_DELAY
    push af                     ; 11E5 F5           Save display/countdown counter
    ld a,($5B68)                ; 11E6 3A 68 5B     A := FLAGN
    bit 1,a                     ; 11E9 CB 4F        Is the NextZXOS flag-1 hook active?
    jr z,`WAIT_KEY_INIT         ; 11EB 28 03        No: use the normal delay
    rst $18                     ; 11ED DF           Call ROM1 routine $3791
    .defw $3791                 ; 11EE 91 37      
`WAIT_KEY_INIT
    ld bc,$0BB8                 ; 11F0 01 B8 0B     BC := repeat delay counter
    pop af                      ; 11F3 F1           Restore countdown counter
`WAIT_KEY_LOOP
    and a                       ; 11F4 A7           Is the countdown disabled?
    jr z,`WAIT_KEY_HALT         ; 11F5 28 0B        Yes: wait for a key
    dec c                       ; 11F7 0D           Delay tick
    jr nz,`WAIT_KEY_HALT        ; 11F8 20 08        Not expired yet
    djnz `WAIT_KEY_HALT         ; 11FA 10 06        Not expired yet
    dec a                       ; 11FC 3D           One countdown unit elapsed
    jp z,L10E5                  ; 11FD CA E5 10     Countdown ended: run screen saver/demo
    jr `WAIT_KEY_DELAY          ; 1200 18 E3        Continue with updated countdown
`WAIT_KEY_HALT
    halt                        ; 1202 76           Wait for next interrupt/key scan
    ld hl,$5C3B                 ; 1203 21 3B 5C     HL := FLAGS
    bit 5,(hl)                  ; 1206 CB 6E        Has a new key been pressed?
    jr z,`WAIT_KEY_LOOP         ; 1208 28 EA        No: keep waiting
    res 5,(hl)                  ; 120A CB AE        Consume the new-key flag
    ld a,($5C08)                ; 120C 3A 08 5C     A := LAST_K
    ld hl,$5C41                 ; 120F 21 41 5C     HL := CDFLAG
    cp $0E                      ; 1212 FE 0E        Mode-switch key?
    jr z,HANDLE_MODE_KEY        ; 1214 28 06        Yes: preserve CDFLAG bit 0
    res 0,(hl)                  ; 1216 CB 86        Clear CDFLAG bit 0 for ordinary keys
    cp $10                      ; 1218 FE 10        Is it outside the mode/control range?
    jr nc,L123B                 ; 121A 30 1F        Yes: return it as a normal key
HANDLE_MODE_KEY
    push af                     ; 121C F5           Save LAST_K while updating mode flags
    cp $06                      ; 121D FE 06      
    jr nz,L122A                 ; 121F 20 09      
    ld hl,$5C6A                 ; 1221 21 6A 5C   
    ld a,$08                    ; 1224 3E 08      
    xor (hl)                    ; 1226 AE         
    ld (hl),a                   ; 1227 77         
    jr L1236                    ; 1228 18 0C      
L122A
    cp $0E                      ; 122A FE 0E      
    jr c,L123A                  ; 122C 38 0C      
    sub $0D                     ; 122E D6 0D      
    cp (hl)                     ; 1230 BE         
    ld (hl),a                   ; 1231 77         
    jr nz,L1236                 ; 1232 20 02      
    ld (hl),$00                 ; 1234 36 00      
L1236
    set 3,(iy+$02)              ; 1236 FD CB 02 DE
L123A
    pop af                      ; 123A F1         
L123B
    bit 7,(iy+$30)              ; 123B FD CB 30 7E
    scf                         ; 123F 37         
    ret nz                      ; 1240 C0         
    bit 1,(iy+$07)              ; 1241 FD CB 07 4E
    ret nz                      ; 1245 C0         
    ld hl,$1264                 ; 1246 21 64 12   
    ld b,$08                    ; 1249 06 08      
L124B
    cp (hl)                     ; 124B BE         
    inc hl                      ; 124C 23         
    jr nz,L1250                 ; 124D 20 01      
    ld a,(hl)                   ; 124F 7E         
L1250
    inc hl                      ; 1250 23         
    djnz L124B                  ; 1251 10 F8      
    scf                         ; 1253 37         
    ret                         ; 1254 C9         
L1255
    call WAIT_KEY               ; 1255 CD E0 11   
    push af                     ; 1258 F5         
    ld a,($5C39)                ; 1259 3A 39 5C   
    ld hl,$00C8                 ; 125C 21 C8 00   
    call PLAY_BEEP_TONE         ; 125F CD 20 3E   
    pop af                      ; 1262 F1         
    ret                         ; 1263 C9         
    add a,$5B                   ; 1264 C6 5B      
    push bc                     ; 1266 C5         
    ld e,l                      ; 1267 5D         
    rst $00                     ; 1268 C7         
    ld a,a                      ; 1269 7F         
    jp po,LC37E                 ; 126A E2 7E C3   
    ld a,h                      ; 126D 7C         
    call LCC5C                  ; 126E CD 5C CC   
    ld a,e                      ; 1271 7B         
    bit 7,l                     ; 1272 CB 7D      
L1274
    ld bc,$1517                 ; 1274 01 17 15   
CLS_LINES
    ld a,c                      ; 1277 79         
    sub b                       ; 1278 90         
    inc a                       ; 1279 3C         
    ld c,a                      ; 127A 4F         
    ld a,$18                    ; 127B 3E 18      
    sub b                       ; 127D 90         
    ld b,a                      ; 127E 47         
L127F
    push bc                     ; 127F C5         
    rst $28                     ; 1280 EF         
    sbc a,e                     ; 1281 9B         
    ld c,$0E                    ; 1282 0E 0E      
    ex af,af'                   ; 1284 08         
    bit 3,(iy+$45)              ; 1285 FD CB 45 5E
    jr z,L12AE                  ; 1289 28 23      
    ex af,af'                   ; 128B 08         
    ld a,$0B                    ; 128C 3E 0B      
    ex af,af'                   ; 128E 08         
    call L12D1                  ; 128F CD D1 12   
L1292
    push hl                     ; 1292 E5         
    xor a                       ; 1293 AF         
    ld b,$20                    ; 1294 06 20      
L1296
    ld (hl),a                   ; 1296 77         
    inc hl                      ; 1297 23         
    djnz L1296                  ; 1298 10 FC      
    pop hl                      ; 129A E1         
    push hl                     ; 129B E5         
    set 5,h                     ; 129C CB EC      
    ld b,$20                    ; 129E 06 20      
L12A0
    ld (hl),a                   ; 12A0 77         
    inc hl                      ; 12A1 23         
    djnz L12A0                  ; 12A2 10 FC      
    pop hl                      ; 12A4 E1         
    inc h                       ; 12A5 24         
    dec c                       ; 12A6 0D         
    jr nz,L1292                 ; 12A7 20 E9      
    call L12D1                  ; 12A9 CD D1 12   
    jr L12BB                    ; 12AC 18 0D      
L12AE
    push hl                     ; 12AE E5         
    ld b,$20                    ; 12AF 06 20      
    xor a                       ; 12B1 AF         
L12B2
    ld (hl),a                   ; 12B2 77         
    inc hl                      ; 12B3 23         
    djnz L12B2                  ; 12B4 10 FC      
    pop hl                      ; 12B6 E1         
    inc h                       ; 12B7 24         
    dec c                       ; 12B8 0D         
    jr nz,L12AE                 ; 12B9 20 F3      
L12BB
    ld b,$20                    ; 12BB 06 20      
    push bc                     ; 12BD C5         
    rst $28                     ; 12BE EF         
    adc a,b                     ; 12BF 88         
    ld c,$EB                    ; 12C0 0E EB      
    pop bc                      ; 12C2 C1         
    ld a,(ATTR_P)               ; 12C3 3A 8D 5C   
L12C6
    ld (hl),a                   ; 12C6 77         
    inc hl                      ; 12C7 23         
    djnz L12C6                  ; 12C8 10 FC      
    pop bc                      ; 12CA C1         
    dec b                       ; 12CB 05         
    dec c                       ; 12CC 0D         
    jr nz,L127F                 ; 12CD 20 B0      
    scf                         ; 12CF 37         
    ret                         ; 12D0 C9         
L12D1
    push af                     ; 12D1 F5         
    push bc                     ; 12D2 C5         
    ld a,$53                    ; 12D3 3E 53      
    call GetNxRegA              ; 12D5 CD DE 12   
    ex af,af'                   ; 12D8 08         
    out (c),a                   ; 12D9 ED 79      
    pop bc                      ; 12DB C1         
    pop af                      ; 12DC F1         
    ret                         ; 12DD C9         
;
; Read the Next register port value addressed by A
;
GetNxRegA
    ld bc,$243B                 ; 12DE 01 3B 24     Register index port
    out (c),a                   ; 12E1 ED 79        A --> Register index
    inc b                       ; 12E3 04           Register value port
    in a,(c)                    ; 12E4 ED 78        Read register value
    ret                         ; 12E6 C9           Done
;
;
;
    inc d                       ; 12E7 14         
    ld bc,$2047                 ; 12E8 01 47 20   
    inc d                       ; 12EB 14         
    nop                         ; 12EC 00         
    add hl,de                   ; 12ED 19         
    or b                        ; 12EE B0         
    ld b,$00                    ; 12EF 06 00      
    ld (hl),l                   ; 12F1 75         
    ld l,c                      ; 12F2 69         
    ld h,h                      ; 12F3 64         
    ld h,l                      ; 12F4 65         
    jr nz,L130B                 ; 12F5 20 14

    push af                     ; 15DD F5           Preserve AF on stack
    ex af,af'                   ; 15DE 08           Swap to alternate AF register
    ret                         ; 15DF C9           Return from subroutine
;
; RST $08 Continuation: Stack Switch and Page RAM Bank 7
; This is the target of RST $08 (JP L15E0 at $0008). It switches the stack pointer to the
; work area (TMPSP, typically $5BFF) and pages RAM bank 7 into $C000-$FFFF.
; Entry: Called via RST $08
; Exit: RAM bank 7 paged at $C000, SP pointing to work area below sysvars, AF preserved via AF'

    ret                         ; 15F6 C9           Return with new stack and ROM page active

L2326
    dec c                       ; 2326 0D         
    jr c,L2329                  ; 2327 38 00      
;
; --- Subroutine: Initialize NextOS structures and copy font data ---
; This routine sets up NextOS data structures in banks $0B and $10, 
; initializes memory regions, and copies/processes font data.
; Entry: B = initialization flag (0 = full init, non-zero = skip init)
; Exit: NextOS structures initialized, font data copied
;
L2329
    push $2386                  ; 2329 ED 8A 23 86  Push return address $2386
    ld e,b                      ; 232D 58           E = initialization flag from B
L232E
    ld d,$54                    ; 232E 16 54        D = $54 (use MMU Slots 4 and 5)
    call PgIn100B               ; 2330 CD 0A 27     Page in banks $0B and $10 to slots 4/5
    dec e                       ; 2333 1D           Decrement init flag
    jr z,L2372                  ; 2334 28 3C        If flag was 1, skip initialization
;
; --- Initialize NextOS data structure at $A000 ---
;
    ld hl,$A000                 ; 2336 21 00 A0     HL = $A000 (start of structure)
    ld de,$A001                 ; 2339 11 01 A0     DE = $A001 (for LDIR fill)
    ld bc,$0151                 ; 233C 01 51 01     BC = $0151 (337 bytes)
    ld (hl),l                   ; 233F 75           Store $00 at $A000
    ldir                        ; 2340 ED B0        Fill $A000-$A151 with $00
    ld a,(LBANK)                ; 2342 3A 69 5B     A = last RAM bank number
    inc a                       ; 2345 3C           A = number of RAM banks
    add a,a                     ; 2346 87           A = number of 8K pages (banks * 2)
    ld ($A050),a                ; 2347 32 50 A0     Store number of pages
    ld a,$10                    ; 234A 3E 10        A = $10
    ld ($A051),a                ; 234C 32 51 A0     Store $10 at $A051
    ld hl,$FFFF                 ; 234F 21 FF FF     HL = $FFFF
    ld ($A000),hl               ; 2352 22 00 A0     Store $FFFF at $A000
    ld ($A028),hl               ; 2355 22 28 A0     Store $FFFF at $A028
    ld a,l                      ; 2358 7D           A = $FF
    ld ($A002),a                ; 2359 32 02 A0     Store $FF at $A002
    ld ($A02A),a                ; 235C 32 2A A0     Store $FF at $A02A
    ld hl,$3FF7                 ; 235F 21 F7 3F     HL = $3FF7
    ld ($A020),hl               ; 2362 22 20 A0     Store $3FF7 at $A020
    ld ($A048),hl               ; 2365 22 48 A0     Store $3FF7 at $A048
    ld hl,$0001                 ; 2368 21 01 00     HL = $0001
    rst $20                     ; 236B E7           Call ROM2 routine
    .defw $01BD                 ; 236C BD 01        ROM2 address: IDE_WINDOW_MENU
    ld a,e                      ; 236E 7B           A = E (init flag)
    ld ($5B58),a                ; 236F 32 58 5B     Store init flag at $5B58
;
; --- Copy and decompress font data ---
;
L2372
    ld hl,$3D00                 ; 2372 21 00 3D     HL = $3D00 (source: font data in ROM)
    ld de,$BC00                 ; 2375 11 00 BC     DE = $BC00 (dest in paged RAM)
    ld bc,$0300                 ; 2378 01 00 03     BC = $0300 (768 bytes)
    rst $28                     ; 237B EF           Call ROM1 routine
    .defw $33C3                 ; 237C C3 33        ROM1 address: copy routine
    call ExpFont                ; 237E CD 9B 23     Process font data
    ld d,$54                    ; 2381 16 54        D = $54 (MMU slot)
    jp L2799                    ; 2383 C3 99 27     Jump to cleanup/return
    call L2708                  ; 2386 CD 08 27     Additional initialization
    call L264D                  ; 2389 CD 4D 26     More initialization
    ld hl,MENU_CHOICE           ; 238C 21 00 F7     HL = MENU_CHOICE
    call L0F54                  ; 238F CD 54 0F     Initialize memory region
    ld hl,$FB00                 ; 2392 21 00 FB     HL = $FB00
    call L0F54                  ; 2395 CD 54 0F     Initialize memory region
    jp L2797                    ; 2398 C3 97 27     Jump to final return
;
; --- Subroutine: Process and expand font data ---
; Converts compressed font data from $BC00 to expanded format at $B800,
; and generates character patterns at $AD00 using lookup table at ChLUT.
; The lookup table contains 9 special characters that use custom pattern bytes
; for their first row (top scanline), while the remaining rows use standard
; pattern data from $23F2. This allows specific characters to have unique
; top row patterns for special symbols, accented characters, or graphical elements.
; Entry: None (expects font data at $BC00)
; Exit: Processed font at $B800, character patterns at $AD00
;
ExpFont
    ld hl,$BC00                 ; 239B 21 00 BC     HL = $BC00 (source: compressed font)
    ld de,$B800                 ; 239E 11 00 B8     DE = $B800 (dest: expanded font)
    ld bc,$0003                 ; 23A1 01 03 00     BC = $0300 (768 bytes)
L23A4
    ld a,(hl)                   ; 23A4 7E           Load byte from compressed font
    inc hl                      ; 23A5 23           Advance source pointer
    add a,a                     ; 23A6 87           Shift left (multiply by 2)
    ld (de),a                   ; 23A7 12           Store expanded byte
    inc de                      ; 23A8 13           Advance dest pointer
    djnz L23A4                  ; 23A9 10 F9        Loop for B bytes
    dec c                       ; 23AB 0D           Decrement page counter
    jr nz,L23A4                 ; 23AC 20 F6        Continue if more pages
    push ix                     ; 23AE DD E5        Save IX
    ld ix,ChLUT                 ; 23B0 DD 21 32 26  IX = ChLUT (lookup table pointer)
    ld hl,$23F2                 ; 23B4 21 F2 23     HL = $23F2 (pattern data)
    ld de,$AD00                 ; 23B7 11 00 AD     DE = $AD00 (character patterns)
    ld b,$60                    ; 23BA 06 60        B = $60 (96 characters)
L23BC
    ld c,$08                    ; 23BC 0E 08        C = $08 (8 rows per character)
L23BE
    xor a                       ; 23BE AF           A = $00 (default pattern byte)
    bit 3,c                     ; 23BF CB 59        Test bit 3 of row counter
    jr nz,L23DA                 ; 23C1 20 17        If set, use default $00
    ld a,c                      ; 23C3 79           A = row counter
    dec a                       ; 23C4 3D           A = row - 1
    jr nz,L23D8                 ; 23C5 20 11        If not first row, use pattern data
    ld a,b                      ; 23C7 78           A = character counter
    cp (ix)                     ; 23C8 DD BE 00     Compare with lookup table entry
    ld a,$00                    ; 23CB 3E 00        A = $00 (default)
    jr nz,L23DA                 ; 23CD 20 0B        If no match, use default
    inc ix                      ; 23CF DD 23        Advance to next lookup entry
    ld a,(ix)                   ; 23D1 DD 7E 00     Load special pattern byte
    inc ix                      ; 23D4 DD 23        Advance lookup pointer
    jr L23DA                    ; 23D6 18 02        Use special pattern
L23D8
    ld a,(hl)                   ; 23D8 7E           Load pattern from data
    inc hl                      ; 23D9 23           Advance pattern pointer
L23DA
    push af                     ; 23DA F5           Save pattern byte
    and $E0                     ; 23DB E6 E0        Mask upper 3 bits
    ld (de),a                   ; 23DD 12           Store in character pattern (byte 0)
    inc d                       ; 23DE 14           D += 1 (skip 768 bytes)
    inc d                       ; 23DF 14           D += 1 (skip 768 bytes)
    inc d                       ; 23E0 14           D += 1 (skip 768 bytes)
    pop af                      ; 23E1 F1           Restore pattern byte
    add a,a                     ; 23E2 87           Shift left 1 bit
    add a,a                     ; 23E3 87           Shift left 1 bit
    add a,a                     ; 23E4 87           Shift left 1 bit
    ld (de),a                   ; 23E5 12           Store in character pattern (byte 1)
    dec d                       ; 23E6 15           Restore D
    dec d                       ; 23E7 15           Restore D
    dec d                       ; 23E8 15           Restore D
    inc de                      ; 23E9 13           Advance to next row
    dec c                       ; 23EA 0D           Decrement row counter
    jr nz,L23BE                 ; 23EB 20 D1        Loop for all 8 rows
    djnz L23BC                  ; 23ED 10 CD        Loop for all 96 characters
    pop ix                      ; 23EF DD E1        Restore IX
    ret                         ; 23F1 C9           Return

;
; --- Lookup table for special character patterns (9 entries x 2 bytes) ---
; Format: character_number, special_pattern_byte
;
ChLUT
    .defb $5C, $42              ; 2632 5C 42      
    .defb $2F, $20              ; 2634 2F 20      
    .defb $21, $FF              ; 2636 21 FF      
    .defb $19, $C6              ; 2638 19 C6      
    .defb $16, $46              ; 263A 16 46      
    .defb $10, $88              ; 263C 10 88      
    .defb $0F, $21              ; 263E 0F 21      
    .defb $07, $C6              ; 2640 07 C6      
    .defb $01, $EE              ; 2642 01 EE      
    call L2708                  ; 2644 CD 08 27   
    call L264D                  ; 2647 CD 4D 26   
    jp L2797                    ; 264A C3 97 27   
L264D

    ld bc,$0014                 ; 2653 01 14 00   
    ldir                        ; 2656 ED B0

    ld de,$FF0D                 ; 2670 11 0D FF   
L2673

;
; Pages in banks $0B to MMU4, $10 to MMU5. Saves old pages to OMMU45
;
PgIn100B
    pop bc                      ; 270A C1           Pop the return value into BC
    ld hl,$0000                 ; 270B 21 00 00   
    add hl,sp                   ; 270E 39           HL := SP, Carry reset
    ld a,h                      ; 270F 7C           A := the MSB of SP
    cp $5C                      ; 2710 FE 5C      
    jr c,L271C                  ; 2712 38 08        Jump if MSP of SP < $5C (temporary stack)
    ld hl,(TMPSP)               ; 2714 2A 6A 5B     HL := TMPSP
    ld (TMPSP),sp               ; 2717 ED 73 6A 5B  Save SP to TMPSP
    ld sp,hl                    ; 271B F9           Use the TMPSP
L271C
    ld ($5B8E),a                ; 271C 32 8E 5B     Store the MSB of SP
    push bc                     ; 271F C5           Store the return value into the stack
    call SetMMU100B             ; 2720 CD BA 27     Set pages $0B for Slot 4, $10 for slot 5
    ld (OMMU45),hl              ; 2723 22 8A 5B     Old Slot4 and 5 pages
    ret                         ; 2726 C9           Done

;
; Sets the MMU slot pair in D ($50-$57) to the ULA Screen banks
;
SetMMU100B
    ld hl,$100B                 ; 27BA 21 0B 10     HL := Pages $10, and $0b
;
; Sets the MMU slot pair in D ($50-$57) to the L, and H values, respectively
; Returns with the old values in HL
;
SetMMU
    push bc                     ; 27BD C5           Save BC
    ld bc,$243B                 ; 27BE 01 3B 24     BC := Next register index port
    out (c),d                   ; 27C1 ED 51        Set thre Next register index to D
    inc b                       ; 27C3 04           BC := Next register value port
    in a,(c)                    ; 27C4 ED 78        A := Register value
    out (c),l                   ; 27C6 ED 69        Set new register value to L
    ld l,a                      ; 27C8 6F           L := old register value
    dec b                       ; 27C9 05           BC := Next register index port
    inc d                       ; 27CA 14           Next register index
    out (c),d                   ; 27CB ED 51        Set thre Next register index to D
    inc b                       ; 27CD 04           BC := Next register value port
    in a,(c)                    ; 27CE ED 78        A := Register value
    out (c),h                   ; 27D0 ED 61        Set new register value to L
    ld h,a                      ; 27D2 67           L := old register value
    pop bc                      ; 27D3 C1           Restore BC
    ret                         ; 27D4 C9           Done
;
;
;

;
; Print the current CPU speed indicator in the startup menu title area.
; DISP_CPUSPEED holds the ROM's saved CPU speed value and indexes short inline strings
; in the data immediately following this routine.
;
SHOW_CPU_SPEED
    ld a,$FF                    ; 3D6D 3E FF      
    call PRINT_MENU_AT          ; 3D6F CD B5 07     Position the speed field
    ld a,(DISP_CPUSPEED)        ; 3D72 3A BB D5   
    add a,$85                   ; 3D75 C6 85      
    ld l,a                      ; 3D77 6F

    ld b,a                      ; 3D7A 47

    ld h,a                      ; 3D7D 67         
    ld c,(hl)                   ; 3D7E 4E         
    add hl,bc                   ; 3D7F 09         
`PRINT_SPEED
    call PRINT_FF_TEXT          ; 3D80 CD 1D 08     Print selected $FF-terminated speed text
    scf                         ; 3D83 37         
    ret                         ; 3D84 C9         
    inc b                       ; 3D85 04         
    dec c                       ; 3D86 0D         
    ld d,$1F                    ; 3D87 16 1F      
    jr nz,L3DBE                 ; 3D89 20 33      
    ld l,$35                    ; 3D8B 2E 35      
    ld c,l                      ; 3D8D 4D         
    ld c,b                      ; 3D8E 48         
    ld a,d                      ; 3D8F 7A         
    jr nz,L3DD0                 ; 3D90 20 3E      
    rst $38                     ; 3D92 FF

    jr nz,L3DB6                 ; 3D94 20 20      
    scf                         ; 3D96 37

    rst $38                     ; 3D9C FF

    jr nz,L3DD1                 ; 3D9E 20 31      
    inc (hl)                    ; 3DA0 34         
    ld c,l                      ; 3DA1 4D         
    ld c,b                      ; 3DA2 48         
    ld a,d                      ; 3DA3 7A         
    jr nz,L3DE4                 ; 3DA4 20 3E      
    rst $38                     ; 3DA6 FF         
    inc a                       ; 3DA7 3C         
    jr nz,L3DDC                 ; 3DA8 20 32

;
; Calls a subroutine in ROM2
; The subroutine address is stored immediately after the RST $20 instruction
;
Rom2Call
    ld (TMPBC),bc               ; 3E00 ED 43 54 5B  Store BC temporarily
    ex (sp),hl                  ; 3E04 E3           HL := return address
    ld c,(hl)                   ; 3E05 4E         
    inc hl                      ; 3E06 23         
    ld b,(hl)                   ; 3E07 46           Now, BC contains the address to call
    inc hl                      ; 3E08 23         
    ex (sp),hl                  ; 3E09 E3           Set the return address (from RST $20)
    push RETF2                  ; 3E0A ED 8A 3E 13  Push the return address (from ROM2 routine)
    push bc                     ; 3E0E C5           This is the address to call in ROM2
    ld bc,(TMPBC)               ; 3E0F ED 4B 54 5B  Restore the original BC value
;
; At the RETF2 address in ROM2 there is a nextreg $8E,$00 instruction that switches back to ROM0
; ROM2 contains a ret instruction after RETF2, and that returns to the caller in ROM0
;
RETF2
    nextreg $8E,$02             ; 3E13 ED 91 8E 02  Switch to ROM2.
    ret                         ; 3E17 C9           Jump to ROM2 routine
;
;
;
;
; Play the standard menu feedback beep.
; Uses the system RASP value as a duration/pitch input and delegates to the
; shared sound/output helper at L3DCD.
;
MENU_BEEP
    ld a,($5C38)                ; 3E18 3A 38 5C   
    srl a                       ; 3E1B CB 3F      
    ld hl,$0C80                 ; 3E1D 21 80 0C   
;
; Play a beep tone using HL and A as sound parameters, preserving IX.
;
PLAY_BEEP_TONE
    push ix                     ; 3E20 DD E5      
    ld d,$00                    ; 3E22 16 00      
    ld e,a                      ; 3E24 5F         
`BEEP_CALL
    call L3DCD                  ; 3E25 CD CD 3D   
    pop ix                      ; 3E28 DD E1      
    ret                         ; 3E2A C9         
    push ix                     ; 3E2B DD E5      
    ld de,$0030                 ; 3E2D 11 30 00   
    ld hl,$0300                 ; 3E30 21 00 03   
    jr `BEEP_CALL               ; 3E33 18 F0      
    push af                     ; 3E35 F5         
    push hl                     ; 3E36 E5         
    ld a,(ATTR_P)               ; 3E37 3A 8D 5C   
    push af                     ; 3E3A F5         
    ld a,(BORDCR)               ; 3E3B 3A 48 5C   
    ld (ATTR_P),a               ; 3E3E 32 8D 5C   
    call L0EC9                  ; 3E41 CD C9 0E   
    ld a,$FD                    ; 3E44 3E FD      
    rst $28                     ; 3E46 EF         
    ld bc,$F116                 ; 3E47 01 16 F1   
    ld (ATTR_P),a               ; 3E4A 32 8D 5C   
    pop hl                      ; 3E4D E1         
L3E4E
    ld a,(hl)                   ; 3E4E 7E         
    inc hl                      ; 3E4F 23         
    cp $FF                      ; 3E50 FE FF      
    jr z,L3E57                  ; 3E52 28 03      
    rst $10                     ; 3E54 D7         
    jr L3E4E                    ; 3E55 18 F7      
L3E57
    pop af                      ; 3E57 F1         
    jr z,L3E69                  ; 3E58 28 0F      
    call WAIT_KEY               ; 3E5A CD E0 11   
L3E5D
    push de                     ; 3E5D D5         
    call L0EC9                  ; 3E5E CD C9 0E   
    ld a,$FE                    ; 3E61 3E FE      
    rst $28                     ; 3E63 EF         
    ld bc,$D116                 ; 3E64 01 16 D1   
    ld a,e                      ; 3E67 7B         
    ret                         ; 3E68 C9         
L3E69
    call WAIT_KEY               ; 3E69 CD E0 11   
    and $DF                     ; 3E6C E6 DF      
    ld e,$00                    ; 3E6E 1E 00      
    cp $43                      ; 3E70 FE 43      
    jr z,L3E5D                  ; 3E72 28 E9      
    inc e                       ; 3E74 1C         
    cp $52                      ; 3E75 FE 52      
    jr z,L3E5D                  ; 3E77 28 E4      
    inc e                       ; 3E79 1C         
    cp $49                      ; 3E7A FE 49      
    jr z,L3E5D                  ; 3E7C 28 DF      
    jr L3E69                    ; 3E7E 18 E9      
;
; Calls a subroutine in ROM2
; The subroutine address is stored immediately after the RST $18 instruction
;
Rom1Call
    ld (TMPBC),bc               ; 3E80 ED 43 54 5B  Store BC temporarily
    ex (sp),hl                  ; 3E84 E3           HL := return address
    ld c,(hl)                   ; 3E85 4E         
    inc hl                      ; 3E86 23         
    ld b,(hl)                   ; 3E87 46           Now, BC contains the address to call
    inc hl                      ; 3E88 23         
    ex (sp),hl                  ; 3E89 E3           Set the return address (from RST $20)
    push $3E93                  ; 3E8A ED 8A 3E 93  Push the return address (from ROM2 routine)
    push bc                     ; 3E8E C5           This is the address to call in ROM2
L3E8F
    ld bc,(TMPBC)               ; 3E8F ED 4B 54 5B  Restore the original BC value
;
; At the RETF1 address in ROM1 there is a nextreg $8E,$00 instruction that switches back to ROM0
; ROM1 contains a ret instruction after RETF1, and that returns to the caller in ROM0
;
RETF1
    nextreg $8E,$01             ; 3E93 ED 91 8E 01  Switch to ROM2.
    ret                         ; 3E97 C9           Jump to ROM2 routine
;
;
;
L3E98
    ld hl,($5C5D)               ; 3E98 2A 5D 5C   
    push hl                     ; 3E9B E5         
    ld hl,($5C51)               ; 3E9C 2A 51 5C   
    add hl,$000F                ; 3E9F ED 34 0F 00
    ld ($5C5D),hl               ; 3EA3 22 5D 5C   
    ex (sp),hl                  ; 3EA6 E3         
    push hl                     ; 3EA7 E5         
    exx                         ; 3EA8 D9
